FEATURE: "Coliaty parcel-change demand" — lets admin/seller file a CORRECT_INFORMATION or CHANGE_DESTINATION request against Coliaty's API for an order's parcel, and on successful CHANGE_DESTINATION flips the order's status to the RELAUNCH_NEW order status.

SURFACES (run `git diff` and `git status` to see everything; the new untracked file is part of the feature):
1. supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts — new ColiatyAdapter methods (~lines 769-891) + two dispatcher cases (~4452-4499): `get_allowed_destination_cities` (GET {base}/parcel/{code}/allowed-destination-cities, defensive normalization to {cities:[{id,name}]}) and `create_parcel_change_demand` (POST {base}/parcel-change-demand/create, body {package_code, request_type, package_phone, package_reciever, package_price, package_note, package_city?, package_address?}).
2. src/components/orders/ParcelChangeDemandDialog.tsx (NEW, untracked — read it in full) — 3-step dialog; note the dispatcher wraps success as {success, data:{...}} and the dialog unwraps `json.data ?? json`; errors are returned UNwrapped {error, status_code, errors?}.
3. src/pages/admin/Orders.tsx + src/pages/seller/Orders.tsx — menu items + `getParcelChangeGate` helpers + dialog mounts; gating = latest COMPANY parcel is a Coliaty provider (matched via provider_type through the companies map) AND package status not terminal (is_terminal || is_returned master flags).
4. src/components/orders/OrdersListView.tsx + SellerOrdersListView.tsx — same menu item via onModifyParcel/getModifyParcelGate props.

KNOWN + ACCEPTED (do not re-report): local-validation errors use the generic {success:false, error} 400 envelope while Coliaty-passthrough errors use {error, status_code, errors?}; custom error responses omit success:false; allowed-cities response shape is undocumented upstream, normalization is best-effort; card menus + list views covered, other views out of scope; tsbuildinfo churn.

FOCUS YOUR ATTACK ON:
- The dialog↔edge contract: every read/write across the seam (cities array, demand.ref, error paths incl. 403 special-case, provider_id propagation).
- The status flip: resolves order_statuses by code RELAUNCH_NEW, updates orders.order_status_id with .select('id') zero-row detection. Can it fire when it shouldn't (CORRECT_INFORMATION path, failed demand), or fail silently?
- The seller gating: can a courier parcel's tracking code ever reach Coliaty? (gate reads order.can_track.provider_id / tracking_code with latest_tracking_code fallback).
- The edge validation: can a request reach Coliaty with missing/invalid fields? request_type whitelist, CHANGE_DESTINATION city+address requirement, CORRECT_INFORMATION omitting city keys.
- React correctness: state reset on open, stale citiesQuery data across orders (queryKey includes providerId+trackingCode?), mutation retry path, kept-mounted query gating.
- Any TypeScript/runtime hazard tsc would miss (unsafe casts, Number('') coercion, null flows).

OUTPUT FORMAT (mandatory): first line exactly one of PASS / FAIL / PASS_WITH_NOTES. Then findings ranked by severity, each with file:line, the concrete failure scenario, and a one-line suggested fix. If you cannot refute anything material, say so explicitly rather than inventing nits.

# Code Reviewer

Complete toolkit for code reviewer with modern tools and best practices.

## Quick Start

### Main Capabilities

This skill provides three core capabilities through automated scripts:

```bash
# Script 1: Pr Analyzer
python scripts/pr_analyzer.py [options]

# Script 2: Code Quality Checker
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py [options]

# Script 3: Review Report Generator
python scripts/review_report_generator.py [options]
```

## Core Capabilities

### 1. Pr Analyzer

Automated tool for pr analyzer tasks.

**Features:**
- Automated scaffolding
- Best practices built-in
- Configurable templates
- Quality checks

**Usage:**
```bash
python scripts/pr_analyzer.py <project-path> [options]
```

### 2. Code Quality Checker

Comprehensive analysis and optimization tool.

**Features:**
- Deep analysis
- Performance metrics
- Recommendations
- Automated fixes

**Usage:**
```bash
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py <target-path> [--verbose]
```

### 3. Review Report Generator

Advanced tooling for specialized tasks.

**Features:**
- Expert-level automation
- Custom configurations
- Integration ready
- Production-grade output

**Usage:**
```bash
python scripts/review_report_generator.py [arguments] [options]
```

## Tech Stack

**Languages:** TypeScript, JavaScript, Python, Go, Swift, Kotlin
**Frontend:** React, Next.js, React Native, Flutter
**Backend:** Node.js, Express, GraphQL, REST APIs
**Database:** PostgreSQL, Prisma, NeonDB, Supabase
**DevOps:** Docker, Kubernetes, Terraform, GitHub Actions, CircleCI
**Cloud:** AWS, GCP, Azure

## Development Workflow

### 1. Setup and Configuration

```bash
# Install dependencies
npm install
# or
pip install -r requirements.txt

# Configure environment
cp .env.example .env
```

### 2. Run Quality Checks

```bash
# Use the analyzer script
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py .

# Review recommendations
# Apply fixes
```

### 3. Implement Best Practices

Follow the patterns and practices documented in:
- `references/code_review_checklist.md`
- `references/coding_standards.md`
- `references/common_antipatterns.md`

## Best Practices Summary

### Code Quality
- Follow established patterns
- Write comprehensive tests
- Document decisions
- Review regularly

### Performance
- Measure before optimizing
- Use appropriate caching
- Optimize critical paths
- Monitor in production

### Security
- Validate all inputs
- Use parameterized queries
- Implement proper authentication
- Keep dependencies updated

### Maintainability
- Write clear code
- Use consistent naming
- Add helpful comments
- Keep it simple

## Common Commands

```bash
# Development
npm run dev
npm run build
npm run test
npm run lint

# Analysis
python scripts/code_quality_checker.py .
python scripts/review_report_generator.py --analyze

# Deployment
docker build -t app:latest .
docker-compose up -d
kubectl apply -f k8s/
```

         return `data:application/pdf;base64,${base64}`;

+
+    // GET /parcel/{code}/allowed-destination-cities. Response shape is undocumented,
+    // so normalize defensively: accepts a top-level array, or an array wrapped in a
+    // `data` key, of either strings or objects with id/name-like keys.
+    async getAllowedDestinationCities(trackingCode) {
+        const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/parcel/${encodeURIComponent(trackingCode)}/allowed-destination-cities`, {
+            headers: { Authorization: this.getAuthHeader() }
+        });
+
+        if (!response.ok) {
+            const errorText = await response.text();
+            console.error('Coliaty allowed-destination-cities error:', response.status, errorText);
+            let errorMsg = errorText;
+            let errors;
+            try {
+                const errorJson = JSON.parse(errorText);
+                errorMsg = errorJson?.message || errorJson?.error || errorText;
+                errors = errorJson?.errors;
+            } catch (parseError) {
+                // errorText is not JSON - keep the raw text as the message
+            }
+            const err: any = new Error(`Coliaty Error: ${errorMsg}`);
+            err.status_code = response.status;
+            if (errors) err.errors = errors;
+            throw err;
+        }
+
+        const responseText = await response.text();
+        let data;
+        try {
+            data = JSON.parse(responseText);
+        } catch (e) {
+            throw new Error(`Coliaty returned non-JSON response: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
+        }
+
+        const raw = Array.isArray(data) ? data : Array.isArray(data?.data) ? data.data : null;
+        if (!raw) {
+            throw new Error(`Coliaty: unrecognized allowed-destination-cities response: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
+        }
+
+        const cities = raw.map((c: any) => {
+            if (typeof c === 'string') return { id: null, name: c };
+            return {
+                id: c?.id ?? c?.city_id ?? null,
+                name: String(c?.name ?? c?.city_name ?? c?.label ?? c?.ville ?? '')
+            };
+        }).filter((c: any) => c.name);
+
+        if (cities.length === 0 && raw.length > 0) {
+            throw new Error(`Coliaty: unrecognized allowed-destination-cities item shape: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
+        }
+
+        return { cities };
+    }
+
+    // POST /parcel-change-demand/create. request_type CORRECT_INFORMATION fixes
+    // phone/receiver/price/note (city+address not required); CHANGE_DESTINATION also
+    // requires package_city + package_address. package_reciever is Coliaty's own
+    // misspelling - sent exactly as they define it.
+    async createParcelChangeDemand(trackingCode, params) {
+        const payload: any = {
+            package_code: trackingCode,
+            request_type: params.request_type,
+            package_phone: params.package_phone,
+            package_reciever: params.package_reciever,
+            package_price: params.package_price,
+            package_note: params.package_note
+        };
+        if (params.request_type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION') {
+            payload.package_city = params.package_city;
+            payload.package_address = params.package_address;
+        }
+
+        const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/parcel-change-demand/create`, {
+            method: 'POST',
+            headers: {
+                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
+                Authorization: this.getAuthHeader()
+            },
+            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
+        });
+
+        if (!response.ok) {
+            const errorText = await response.text();
+            console.error('Coliaty parcel-change-demand error:', response.status, errorText);
+            let errorMsg = errorText;
+            let errors;
+            try {
+                const errorJson = JSON.parse(errorText);
+                errorMsg = errorJson?.message || errorJson?.error || errorText;
+                errors = errorJson?.errors;
+            } catch (parseError) {
+                // errorText is not JSON - keep the raw text as the message
+            }
+            const err: any = new Error(`Coliaty Error: ${errorMsg}`);
+            err.status_code = response.status;
+            if (errors) err.errors = errors;
+            throw err;
+        }
+
+        const responseText = await response.text();
+        let data;
+        try {
+            data = JSON.parse(responseText);
+        } catch (e) {
+            throw new Error(`Coliaty returned non-JSON response: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
+        }
+
+        const details = data?.data?.details || {};
+        return {
+            success: true,
+            demand: {
+                id: details.id,
+                ref: details.ref,
+                status: details.status,
+                request_type: details.request_type,
+                created_at: details.created_at
+            },
+            package_code: data?.data?.package_code,
+            package_id: data?.data?.package_id,
+            message: data?.message
+        };
+    }
 }

    74	class EdgeError extends Error {
    75	    status: number;
    76	    constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    77	        super(message);
    78	        this.name = 'EdgeError';
    79	        this.status = status;
    80	    }
    81	}
    82	
    83	const FORBIDDEN_MSG =
    84	    'La clé API Coliaty de ce fournisseur n’a pas la permission de créer des demandes de modification. Contactez Coliaty pour l’activer.';
    85	
    86	export function ParcelChangeDemandDialog({ open, onOpenChange, order, onSuccess }: Props) {
    87	    const { toast } = useToast();
    88	
    89	    const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(0);
    90	    const [type, setType] = useState<DemandType | null>(null);
    91	    const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
    92	    const [receiver, setReceiver] = useState('');
    93	    const [price, setPrice] = useState('');
    94	    const [note, setNote] = useState('');
    95	    const [city, setCity] = useState('');
    96	    const [address, setAddress] = useState('');
    97	
    98	    const trackingCode = order?.trackingCode ?? '';
    99	    const providerId = order?.providerId ?? '';
   100	
   101	    // Fresh prefill each time the dialog opens on an order; full reset on close.
   102	    useEffect(() => {
   103	        if (!open) return;
   104	        setStep(0);
   105	        setType(null);
   106	        setPhone(order?.phone ? String(order.phone) : '');
   107	        setReceiver(order?.name ? String(order.name) : '');
   108	        setPrice(order?.price === null || order?.price === undefined ? '' : String(order.price));
   109	        setNote('');
   110	        setCity('');
   111	        setAddress('');
   112	    }, [open, order?.id]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   113	
   114	    const postEdge = async (action: string, data: Record<string, unknown>) => {
   115	        const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
   116	        if (!session?.access_token) throw new EdgeError('Session expirée — reconnectez-vous.', 401);
   117	        const res = await fetch(EDGE_FUNCTION_URL, {
   118	            method: 'POST',
   119	            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${session.access_token}` },
   120	            body: JSON.stringify({ action, provider_id: providerId, data }),
   121	        });
   122	        const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({} as Record<string, unknown>));
   123	        if (!res.ok) {
   124	            const msg = res.status === 403
   125	                ? FORBIDDEN_MSG
   126	                : (typeof (json as { error?: unknown }).error === 'string' && (json as { error: string }).error)
   127	                    || 'Échec de la requête Coliaty.';
   128	            throw new EdgeError(msg, res.status);
   129	        }
   130	        return json as Record<string, unknown>;
   131	    };
   132	
   133	    // OR-11: kept-mounted dialog — every query here MUST be gated on `open`, or it
   134	    // fires on every page load of every host page with the dialog closed.
   135	    const citiesQuery = useQuery({
   136	        queryKey: ['coliaty-allowed-cities', providerId, trackingCode],
   137	        queryFn: async () => {
   138	            const json = await postEdge('get_allowed_destination_cities', { tracking_code: trackingCode });
   139	            // Success responses are wrapped `{ success: true, data: {...} }` by the
   140	            // dispatcher; errors are NOT wrapped (read at top level in postEdge above).
   141	            const payload = ((json as { data?: Record<string, unknown> }).data ?? json) as Record<string, unknown>;
   142	            return ((payload.cities ?? []) as AllowedCity[]);
   143	        },
   144	        enabled: open && step === 1 && type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' && !!trackingCode && !!providerId,
   145	        staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
   146	        retry: false,
   147	    });
   148	
   149	    const submit = useMutation({
   150	        mutationFn: async (): Promise<{ ref: string; partial: boolean }> => {
   151	            if (!order || !type) throw new EdgeError('Commande introuvable.', 400);
   152	
   153	            const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
   154	                tracking_code: trackingCode,
   155	                request_type: type,
   156	                package_phone: phone.trim(),
   157	                // `package_reciever` is Coliaty's own spelling — send it verbatim.
   158	                package_reciever: receiver.trim(),
   159	                package_price: Number(price),
   160	                package_note: note.trim(),
   161	            };
   162	            if (type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION') {
   163	                const picked = (citiesQuery.data ?? []).find(
   164	                    (c) => String(c.id ?? c.name) === city,
   165	                );
   166	                payload.package_city = picked ? (picked.id ?? picked.name) : city;
   167	                payload.package_address = address.trim();
   168	            }
   169	
   170	            const json = await postEdge('create_parcel_change_demand', payload);
   171	            const respPayload = ((json as { data?: Record<string, unknown> }).data ?? json) as Record<string, unknown>;
   172	            const demand = (respPayload.demand ?? {}) as { ref?: string | number };
   173	            const ref = demand.ref !== undefined && demand.ref !== null ? String(demand.ref) : '—';
   174	
   175	            if (type !== 'CHANGE_DESTINATION') return { ref, partial: false };
   176	
   177	            // Resolve by CODE — never hardcode the status UUID. DB triggers own the
   178	            // stock hold and the history line.
   179	            try {
   180	                const { data: st, error: stErr } = await supabase
   181	                    .from('order_statuses')
   182	                    .select('id')
   183	                    .eq('code', 'RELAUNCH_NEW')
   184	                    .maybeSingle();
   185	                if (stErr || !st?.id) return { ref, partial: true };
   186	
   187	                const { data: updRows, error: updErr } = await supabase
   188	                    .from('orders')
   189	                    .update({ order_status_id: st.id })
   190	                    .eq('id', order.id)
   191	                    .select('id');
   192	                if (updErr || !updRows?.length) return { ref, partial: true };
   193	            } catch {
   194	                return { ref, partial: true };
   195	            }
   196	            return { ref, partial: false };
   197	        },
   198	        onSuccess: (res) => {
   199	            toast({
   200	                title: res.partial ? 'Demande créée — statut non mis à jour' : 'Demande créée',
   201	                description: `Réf ${res.ref}`,
   202	                variant: res.partial ? 'destructive' : undefined,
   203	            });
   204	            onSuccess?.();
   205	        },
   206	        onError: (e: unknown) => {
   207	            toast({
   208	                title: 'Demande refusée',
   209	                description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Échec de la demande Coliaty.',
   210	                variant: 'destructive',
   211	            });
   212	        },
   213	    });
   214	
   215	    const priceNum = Number(price);
   216	    const formValid =
   217	        phone.trim().length > 0
   218	        && receiver.trim().length > 0
   219	        && price.trim().length > 0
   220	        && Number.isFinite(priceNum)
   221	        && priceNum >= 0
   222	        && (type !== 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' || (city.length > 0 && address.trim().length > 0));
   223	
   224	    const stepValid = step === 0 ? !!type : step === 1 ? formValid : false;
   225	
   226	    const goToResult = () => {
   227	        setStep(2);
   228	        submit.reset();
   229	        submit.mutate();
   230	    };
   231	
   232	    const submitError = submit.error;
   233	    const errorMessage = submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : null;
   234	
   235	    return (
   236	        <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
   237	            <DialogContent className="max-w-2xl p-0 border border-ant-line bg-ant-paper rounded-[20px] shadow-[0_32px_90px_-28px_rgba(20,20,19,0.4)] overflow-hidden max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col">
   238	                {/* Gradient hairline */}
   239	                <div className="h-[3px] w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-ant-orange via-ant-orange/60 to-transparent" />
   240	
   241	                <DialogHeader className="px-6 pt-5 pb-5 border-b border-ant-line space-y-4">
   242	                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
   243	                        <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-ant-ink text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
   244	                            <PackageOpen className="w-5 h-5" strokeWidth={1.75} />
   245	                        </div>
   246	                        <div className="min-w-0">
   247	                            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-ant-mid">
   248	                                <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-ant-orange" />
   249	                                Demande Coliaty
   250	                            </div>
   251	                            <DialogTitle className="font-display text-[20px] leading-tight tracking-tight text-ant-ink mt-1">
   252	                                {STEP_TITLES[step].title}
   253	                            </DialogTitle>
   254	                            <DialogDescription className="font-serif italic text-[12.5px] text-ant-mid mt-0.5">
   255	                                {STEP_TITLES[step].desc}
   256	                            </DialogDescription>
   257	                        </div>
   258	                    </div>
   259	
   260	                    {/* Stepper */}
   261	                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   262	                        {STEPS.map((s, i) => {
   263	                            const Icon = s.icon;
   264	                            const done = i < step;
   265	                            const current = i === step;
   266	                            return (
   267	                                <div key={s.label} className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1 last:flex-none">
   268	                                    <div className={cn(
   269	                                        'inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-2.5 h-7 rounded-full border text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] transition',
   270	                                        current ? 'bg-ant-ink text-white border-ant-ink'
   271	                                            : done ? 'bg-ant-green/10 text-ant-green border-ant-green/30'
   272	                                            : 'bg-ant-light text-ant-mid border-ant-line',
   273	                                    )}>
   274	                                        {done ? <Check className="w-3 h-3" /> : <Icon className="w-3 h-3" />}
   275	                                        {s.label}
   276	                                    </div>
   277	                                    {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (
   278	                                        <div className={cn('h-px flex-1 transition', done ? 'bg-ant-green/40' : 'bg-ant-line')} />
   279	                                    )}
   280	                                </div>
   281	                            );
   282	                        })}
   283	                    </div>
   284	                </DialogHeader>
   285	
   286	                <div className="ant-scroll flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto px-6 py-6">
   287	                    {/* ── STEP 0 — TYPE ───────────────────────────────────────── */}
   288	                    {step === 0 && (
   289	                        <div className="space-y-4">
   290	                            {/* Parcel context */}
   291	                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap text-[11.5px] text-ant-mid">
   292	                                <span className="font-semibold text-ant-ink tabular-nums">{trackingCode || '—'}</span>
   293	                                {order?.statusLabel && (
   294	                                    <span
   295	                                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide"
   296	                                        style={{
   297	                                            backgroundColor: order.statusColor ? `${order.statusColor}12` : 'rgba(0,31,63,0.04)',
   298	                                            color: order.statusColor || 'rgba(0,31,63,0.5)',
   299	                                        }}
   300	                                    >
   301	                                        {order.statusLabel}
   302	                                    </span>
   303	                                )}
   304	                            </div>
   305	
   306	                            <div className="space-y-2.5" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Type de demande">
   307	                                <button
   308	                                    type="button"
   309	                                    role="radio"
   310	                                    aria-checked={type === 'CORRECT_INFORMATION'}
   311	                                    onClick={() => setType('CORRECT_INFORMATION')}
   312	                                    className={cn(
   313	                                        'w-full rounded-xl p-4 text-left border transition cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ant-ink/25',
   314	                                        type === 'CORRECT_INFORMATION' ? 'border-ant-ink bg-ant-ink/5' : 'border-ant-line hover:border-ant-ink/20',
   315	                                    )}
   316	                                >
   317	                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-ant-ink">
   318	                                        <PencilLine className="w-4 h-4" /> Corriger les informations
   319	                                    </span>

   324

   329	                                    onClick={() => setType('CHANGE_DESTINATION')}
   330	                                    className={cn(
   331	                                        'w-full rounded-xl p-4 text-left border transition cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ant-ink/25',
   332	                                        type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' ? 'border-ant-ink bg-ant-ink/5' : 'border-ant-line hover:border-ant-ink/20',
   333	                                    )}
   334	                                >
   335	                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] font-semibold text-ant-ink">
   336	                                        <MapPin className="w-4 h-4" /> Changer la destination

   340	                                    </span>

   343	                        </div>

   347	                    {step === 1 && (

   357	                                    />

   360	                                    <Label htmlFor="pcd-receiver" className="text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-ant-ink">Destinataire</Label>

   366	                                    />
   367	                                </div>
   368	                                <div className="space-y-1.5">
   369	                                    <Label htmlFor="pcd-price" className="text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-ant-ink">Prix (DH)</Label>
   370	                                    <Input
   371	                                        id="pcd-price"
   372	                                        type="number"
   373	                                        min={0}
   374	                                        step="0.01"
   375	                                        value={price}
   376	                                        onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)}
   377	                                        className="h-10 rounded-xl border-ant-line text-[12.5px] text-ant-ink tabular-nums"
   378	                                    />
   379	                                </div>
   380	                            </div>
   381	
   382	                            {type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' && (

   386	                                        {citiesQuery.isPending ? (
   387	                                            <div className="space-y-2">

   394	                                                    <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mt-px shrink-0" />
   395	                                                    {citiesQuery.error instanceof Error ? citiesQuery.error.message : 'Échec du chargement des villes.'}
   396	                                                </p>
   397	                                                <Button
   398	                                                    variant="ghost"
   399	                                                    onClick={() => void citiesQuery.refetch()}
   400	                                                    className="h-8 px-3 rounded-full text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-ant-ink hover:bg-ant-ink/5"
   401	                                                >
   402	                                                    Réessayer
   403	                                                </Button>
   404	                                            </div>
   405	                                        ) : (
   406	                                            <Select value={city} onValueChange={setCity}>

   515	                            >

     2	 * ParcelChangeDemandDialog — Coliaty parcel-change demand, 3 steps.
     3	 *
     4	 *   0. Type de demande — correction d'informations OU changement de destination
     5	 *   1. Formulaire      — champs préremplis depuis la commande (+ ville/adresse)
     6	 *   2. Résultat        — envoi, succès / succès partiel / erreur
     7	 *
     8	 * Shared by the admin and the seller Orders pages. The host owns the open state
     9	 * (exactly like `parcelTrackingOpen` / `setParcelTrackingOrderId`) and passes the
    10	 * minimal order slice plus an `onSuccess` callback for its own invalidations.
    11	 */
    12	
    13	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    14	import { useMutation, useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
    15	import {
    16	    AlertCircle, ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, Check, ListChecks, Loader2, MapPin,
    17	    PackageOpen, PencilLine, Send, type LucideIcon,
    18	} from 'lucide-react';
    19	import {
    20	    Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle,
    21	} from '@/components/ui/dialog';
    22	import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
    23	import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
    24	import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
    25	import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
    26	import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton';
    27	import {
    28	    Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue,
    29	} from '@/components/ui/select';
    30	import { useToast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';
    31	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
    32	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
    33	
    34	const EDGE_FUNCTION_URL = `${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/provider-integration`;
    35	
    36	/** Minimal order slice the dialog needs — built by the host at click time. */
    37	export type ParcelChangeDemandOrder = {
    38	    id: string;
    39	    name?: string | null;
    40	    phone?: string | null;
    41	    price?: number | string | null;
    42	    /** Latest trackable company parcel */
    43	    trackingCode: string;
    44	    providerId: string;
    45	    /** Resolved master package status of the parcel (context line only) */
    46	    statusLabel?: string | null;
    47	    statusColor?: string | null;
    48	};
    49	
    50	type Props = {
    51	    open: boolean;
    52	    onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
    53	    order: ParcelChangeDemandOrder | null;
    54	    /** Host-owned invalidation of its orders list (+ KPI) query keys. */
    55	    onSuccess?: () => void;
    56	};
    57	
    58	type Step = 0 | 1 | 2;
    59	type DemandType = 'CORRECT_INFORMATION' | 'CHANGE_DESTINATION';
    60	type AllowedCity = { id: number | null; name: string };
    61	
    62	const STEPS: { label: string; icon: LucideIcon }[] = [
    63	    { label: 'Type', icon: ListChecks },
    64	    { label: 'Détails', icon: PencilLine },
    65	    { label: 'Envoi', icon: Send },
    66	];
    67	
    68	const STEP_TITLES: { title: string; desc: string }[] = [
    69	    { title: 'Type de demande', desc: 'Que faut-il modifier sur ce colis ?' },
    70	    { title: 'Détails de la demande', desc: 'Vérifiez les informations avant l’envoi.' },
    71	    { title: 'Envoi de la demande', desc: 'Transmission à Coliaty.' },
    72	];
    73	
    74	class EdgeError extends Error {
    75	    status: number;
    76	    constructor(message: string, status: number) {
    77	        super(message);
    78	        this.name = 'EdgeError';
    79	        this.status = status;
    80	    }
    81	}
    82	
    83	const FORBIDDEN_MSG =
    84	    'La clé API Coliaty de ce fournisseur n’a pas la permission de créer des demandes de modification. Contactez Coliaty pour l’activer.';
    85	
    86	export function ParcelChangeDemandDialog({ open, onOpenChange, order, onSuccess }: Props) {
    87	    const { toast } = useToast();
    88	
    89	    const [step, setStep] = useState<Step>(0);
    90	    const [type, setType] = useState<DemandType | null>(null);
    91	    const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
    92	    const [receiver, setReceiver] = useState('');
    93	    const [price, setPrice] = useState('');
    94	    const [note, setNote] = useState('');
    95	    const [city, setCity] = useState('');
    96	    const [address, setAddress] = useState('');
    97	
    98	    const trackingCode = order?.trackingCode ?? '';
    99	    const providerId = order?.providerId ?? '';
   100	
   101	    // Fresh prefill each time the dialog opens on an order; full reset on close.
   102	    useEffect(() => {
   103	        if (!open) return;
   104	        setStep(0);
   105	        setType(null);
   106	        setPhone(order?.phone ? String(order.phone) : '');
   107	        setReceiver(order?.name ? String(order.name) : '');
   108	        setPrice(order?.price === null || order?.price === undefined ? '' : String(order.price));
   109	        setNote('');
   110	        setCity('');
   111	        setAddress('');
   112	    }, [open, order?.id]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   113	
   114	    const postEdge = async (action: string, data: Record<string, unknown>) => {
   115	        const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
   116	        if (!session?.access_token) throw new EdgeError('Session expirée — reconnectez-vous.', 401);
   117	        const res = await fetch(EDGE_FUNCTION_URL, {
   118	            method: 'POST',
   119	            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${session.access_token}` },
   120	            body: JSON.stringify({ action, provider_id: providerId, data }),
   121	        });
   122	        const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({} as Record<string, unknown>));
   123	        if (!res.ok) {
   124	            const msg = res.status === 403
   125	                ? FORBIDDEN_MSG
   126	                : (typeof (json as { error?: unknown }).error === 'string' && (json as { error: string }).error)
   127	                    || 'Échec de la requête Coliaty.';
   128	            throw new EdgeError(msg, res.status);
   129	        }
   130	        return json as Record<string, unknown>;
   131	    };
   132	
   133	    // OR-11: kept-mounted dialog — every query here MUST be gated on `open`, or it
   134	    // fires on every page load of every host page with the dialog closed.
   135	    const citiesQuery = useQuery({
   136	        queryKey: ['coliaty-allowed-cities', providerId, trackingCode],
   137	        queryFn: async () => {
   138	            const json = await postEdge('get_allowed_destination_cities', { tracking_code: trackingCode });
   139	            // Success responses are wrapped `{ success: true, data: {...} }` by the
   140	            // dispatcher; errors are NOT wrapped (read at top level in postEdge above).
   141	            const payload = ((json as { data?: Record<string, unknown> }).data ?? json) as Record<string, unknown>;
   142	            return ((payload.cities ?? []) as AllowedCity[]);
   143	        },
   144	        enabled: open && step === 1 && type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' && !!trackingCode && !!providerId,
   145	        staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
   146	        retry: false,
   147	    });
   148	
   149	    const submit = useMutation({
   150	        mutationFn: async (): Promise<{ ref: string; partial: boolean }> => {
   151	            if (!order || !type) throw new EdgeError('Commande introuvable.', 400);
   152	
   153	            const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
   154	                tracking_code: trackingCode,
   155	                request_type: type,
   156	                package_phone: phone.trim(),
   157	                // `package_reciever` is Coliaty's own spelling — send it verbatim.
   158	                package_reciever: receiver.trim(),
   159	                package_price: Number(price),
   160	                package_note: note.trim(),
   161	            };
   162	            if (type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION') {
   163	                const picked = (citiesQuery.data ?? []).find(
   164	                    (c) => String(c.id ?? c.name) === city,
   165	                );
   166	                payload.package_city = picked ? (picked.id ?? picked.name) : city;
   167	                payload.package_address = address.trim();
   168	            }
   169	
   170	            const json = await postEdge('create_parcel_change_demand', payload);
   171	            const respPayload = ((json as { data?: Record<string, unknown> }).data ?? json) as Record<string, unknown>;
   172	            const demand = (respPayload.demand ?? {}) as { ref?: string | number };
   173	            const ref = demand.ref !== undefined && demand.ref !== null ? String(demand.ref) : '—';
   174	
   175	            if (type !== 'CHANGE_DESTINATION') return { ref, partial: false };
   176	
   177	            // Resolve by CODE — never hardcode the status UUID. DB triggers own the
   178	            // stock hold and the history line.
   179	            try {
   180	                const { data: st, error: stErr } = await supabase
   181	                    .from('order_statuses')
   182	                    .select('id')
   183	                    .eq('code', 'RELAUNCH_NEW')
   184	                    .maybeSingle();
   185	                if (stErr || !st?.id) return { ref, partial: true };
   186	
   187	                const { data: updRows, error: updErr } = await supabase
   188	                    .from('orders')
   189	                    .update({ order_status_id: st.id })
   190	                    .eq('id', order.id)
   191	                    .select('id');
   192	                if (updErr || !updRows?.length) return { ref, partial: true };
   193	            } catch {
   194	                return { ref, partial: true };
   195	            }
   196	            return { ref, partial: false };
   197	        },
   198	        onSuccess: (res) => {
   199	            toast({
   200	                title: res.partial ? 'Demande créée — statut non mis à jour' : 'Demande créée',
   201	                description: `Réf ${res.ref}`,
   202	                variant: res.partial ? 'destructive' : undefined,
   203	            });
   204	            onSuccess?.();
   205	        },
   206	        onError: (e: unknown) => {
   207	            toast({
   208	                title: 'Demande refusée',
   209	                description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Échec de la demande Coliaty.',
   210	                variant: 'destructive',
   211	            });
   212	        },
   213	    });
   214	
   215	    const priceNum = Number(price);
   216	    const formValid =
   217	        phone.trim().length > 0
   218	        && receiver.trim().length > 0
   219	        && price.trim().length > 0
   220	        && Number.isFinite(priceNum)
   221	        && priceNum >= 0
   222	        && (type !== 'CHANGE_DESTINATION' || (city.length > 0 && address.trim().length > 0));
   223	
   224	    const stepValid = step === 0 ? !!type : step === 1 ? formValid : false;
   225	
   226	    const goToResult = () => {
   227	        setStep(2);
   228	        submit.reset();
   229	        submit.mutate();
   230	    };
   231	
   232	    const submitError = submit.error;
   233	    const errorMessage = submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : null;
   234	
   235	    return (
   236	        <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
   237	            <DialogContent className="max-w-2xl p-0 border border-ant-line bg-ant-paper rounded-[20px] shadow-[0_32px_90px_-28px_rgba(20,20,19,0.4)] overflow-hidden max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col">
   238	                {/* Gradient hairline */}
   239	                <div className="h-[3px] w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-ant-orange via-ant-orange/60 to-transparent" />
   240	
   241	                <DialogHeader className="px-6 pt-5 pb-5 border-b border-ant-line space-y-4">
   242	                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
   243	                        <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-ant-ink text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm">
   244	                            <PackageOpen className="w-5 h-5" strokeWidth={1.75} />
   245	                        </div>
   246	                        <div className="min-w-0">
   247	                            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-ant-mid">
   248	                                <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-ant-orange" />
   249	                                Demande Coliaty
   250	                            </div>
   251	                            <DialogTitle className="font-display text-[20px] leading-tight tracking-tight text-ant-ink mt-1">
   252	                                {STEP_TITLES[step].title}
   253	                            </DialogTitle>
   254	                            <DialogDescription className="font-serif italic text-[12.5px] text-ant-mid mt-0.5">
   255	                                {STEP_TITLES[step].desc}
   256	                            </DialogDescription>
   257	                        </div>
   258	                    </div>
   259	
   260	                    {/* Stepper */}
   500	                <div className="px-6 py-4 border-t border-ant-line bg-ant-paper">
   501	                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   502	                        {step === 1 ? (
   503	                            <Button
   504	                                variant="ghost"
   505	                                onClick={() => setStep(0)}
   506	                                className="h-10 px-4 rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold text-ant-mid hover:text-ant-ink gap-1.5"
   507	                            >
   508	                                <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> {STEPS[0].label}
   509	                            </Button>
   510	                        ) : step === 2 && submit.isError ? (
   511	                            <Button
   512	                                variant="ghost"
   513	                                onClick={() => { submit.reset(); setStep(1); }}
   514	                                className="h-10 px-4 rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold text-ant-mid hover:text-ant-ink gap-1.5"
   515	                            >
   516	                                <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" /> Retour
   517	                            </Button>
   518	                        ) : <span />}
   519	
   520	                        {step < 2 ? (
   521	                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   522	                                <Button
   523	                                    variant="ghost"
   524	                                    onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
   525	                                    className="h-10 px-4 rounded-full text-[12px] font-semibold text-ant-mid hover:text-ant-ink"
   526	                                >
   527	                                    Fermer
   528	                                </Button>
   529	                                <Button
   530	                                    onClick={() => { if (!stepValid) return; if (step === 0) setStep(1); else goToResult(); }}
   531	                                    disabled={!stepValid}
   532	                                    className="h-10 px-5 rounded-full bg-ant-ink hover:bg-ant-ink/90 text-white text-[12px] font-semibold gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
   533	                                >
   534	                                    Continuer <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
   535	                                </Button>
   536	                            </div>
   537	                        ) : (
   538	                            <Button
   539	                                onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
   540	                                disabled={submit.isPending}
   541	                                className="h-10 px-5 rounded-full bg-ant-ink hover:bg-ant-ink/90 text-white text-[12px] font-semibold disabled:opacity-50"
   542	                            >
   543	                                Fermer
   544	                            </Button>
   545	                        )}
   546	                    </div>
   547	                </div>
   548	            </DialogContent>
   549	        </Dialog>
   550	    );
   551	}
   552	
   553	export default ParcelChangeDemandDialog;
   720	                statusLabel: status.label,
   721	                statusColor: null, // Coliaty doesn't provide color in this endpoint
   722	                receiver: parcel.receiver,
   723	                phone: parcel.phone,
   724	                price: parcel.price,
   725	                comment: status.comment || '',
   726	                date: status.date,
   727	                _original: result.data
   728	            };
   729	        }
   730	
   731	        return null;
   732	    }
   733	
   734	    async getPrintUrl(deliveryNoteRef, labelFormat, printType = 'labels') {
   735	        console.log('🖨️ Coliaty: Getting print PDF for pickup note:', deliveryNoteRef);
   736	        console.log('Format:', labelFormat, '(public API has a single label layout — A4 variant not available)');
   737	        console.log('Print type:', printType, '(ignored - Coliaty returns PDF labels)');
   738	
   739	        // Public API: single labels endpoint (no -a4 variant), Bearer public:secret.
   740	        // Returns the PDF directly (Content-Type application/pdf), same as before.
   741	        const endpoint = `${this.baseUrl}/pickup-note/${deliveryNoteRef}/generate-labels`;
   742	
   743	        const response = await fetch(endpoint, {
   744	            method: 'GET',
   745	            headers: {
   746	                'Authorization': this.getAuthHeader(),
   747	                'Accept': 'application/pdf'
   748	            }
   749	        });
   750	
   751	        if (!response.ok) {
   752	            const errorText = await response.text();
   753	            console.error('Coliaty print error:', errorText);
   754	            throw new Error(`Failed to generate pickup note labels: ${errorText}`);
   755	        }
   756	
   757	        // Get PDF as blob
   758	        const pdfBlob = await response.blob();
   759	
   760	        // Convert blob to base64
   761	        const arrayBuffer = await pdfBlob.arrayBuffer();
   762	        const base64 = btoa(
   763	            new Uint8Array(arrayBuffer)
   764	                .reduce((data, byte) => data + String.fromCharCode(byte), '')
   765	        );
   766	
   767	        return `data:application/pdf;base64,${base64}`;
   768	    }
   769	
   770	    // GET /parcel/{code}/allowed-destination-cities. Response shape is undocumented,
   771	    // so normalize defensively: accepts a top-level array, or an array wrapped in a
   772	    // `data` key, of either strings or objects with id/name-like keys.
   773	    async getAllowedDestinationCities(trackingCode) {
   774	        const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/parcel/${encodeURIComponent(trackingCode)}/allowed-destination-cities`, {
   775	            headers: { Authorization: this.getAuthHeader() }
   776	        });
   777	
   778	        if (!response.ok) {
   779	            const errorText = await response.text();
   780	            console.error('Coliaty allowed-destination-cities error:', response.status, errorText);
   781	            let errorMsg = errorText;
   782	            let errors;
   783	            try {
   784	                const errorJson = JSON.parse(errorText);
   785	                errorMsg = errorJson?.message || errorJson?.error || errorText;
   786	                errors = errorJson?.errors;
   787	            } catch (parseError) {
   788	                // errorText is not JSON - keep the raw text as the message
   789	            }
   790	            const err: any = new Error(`Coliaty Error: ${errorMsg}`);
   791	            err.status_code = response.status;
   792	            if (errors) err.errors = errors;
   793	            throw err;
   794	        }
   795	
   796	        const responseText = await response.text();
   797	        let data;
   798	        try {
   799	            data = JSON.parse(responseText);
   800	        } catch (e) {
   801	            throw new Error(`Coliaty returned non-JSON response: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
   802	        }
   803	
   804	        const raw = Array.isArray(data) ? data : Array.isArray(data?.data) ? data.data : null;
   805	        if (!raw) {
   806	            throw new Error(`Coliaty: unrecognized allowed-destination-cities response: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
   807	        }
   808	
   809	        const cities = raw.map((c: any) => {
   810	            if (typeof c === 'string') return { id: null, name: c };
   811	            return {
   812	                id: c?.id ?? c?.city_id ?? null,
   813	                name: String(c?.name ?? c?.city_name ?? c?.label ?? c?.ville ?? '')
   814	            };
   815	        }).filter((c: any) => c.name);
   816	
   817	        if (cities.length === 0 && raw.length > 0) {
   818	            throw new Error(`Coliaty: unrecognized allowed-destination-cities item shape: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
   819	        }
   820	
   821	        return { cities };
   822	    }
   823	
   824	    // POST /parcel-change-demand/create. request_type CORRECT_INFORMATION fixes
   825	    // phone/receiver/price/note (city+address not required); CHANGE_DESTINATION also
   826	    // requires package_city + package_address. package_reciever is Coliaty's own
   827	    // misspelling - sent exactly as they define it.
   828	    async createParcelChangeDemand(trackingCode, params) {
   829	        const payload: any = {
   830	            package_code: trackingCode,
   831	            request_type: params.request_type,
   832	            package_phone: params.package_phone,
   833	            package_reciever: params.package_reciever,
   834	            package_price: params.package_price,
   835	            package_note: params.package_note
   836	        };
   837	        if (params.request_type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION') {
   838	            payload.package_city = params.package_city;
   839	            payload.package_address = params.package_address;
   840	        }
   841	
   842	        const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/parcel-change-demand/create`, {
   843	            method: 'POST',
   844	            headers: {
   845	                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   846	                Authorization: this.getAuthHeader()
   847	            },
   848	            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
   849	        });
   850	
   851	        if (!response.ok) {
   852	            const errorText = await response.text();
   853	            console.error('Coliaty parcel-change-demand error:', response.status, errorText);
   854	            let errorMsg = errorText;
   855	            let errors;
   856	            try {
   857	                const errorJson = JSON.parse(errorText);
   858	                errorMsg = errorJson?.message || errorJson?.error || errorText;
   859	                errors = errorJson?.errors;
   860	            } catch (parseError) {
   861	                // errorText is not JSON - keep the raw text as the message
   862	            }
   863	            const err: any = new Error(`Coliaty Error: ${errorMsg}`);
   864	            err.status_code = response.status;
   865	            if (errors) err.errors = errors;
   866	            throw err;
   867	        }
   868	
   869	        const responseText = await response.text();
   870	        let data;
   871	        try {
   872	            data = JSON.parse(responseText);
   873	        } catch (e) {
   874	            throw new Error(`Coliaty returned non-JSON response: ${responseText.substring(0, 300)}`);
   875	        }
   876	
   877	        const details = data?.data?.details || {};
   878	        return {
   879	            success: true,
   880	            demand: {
   881	                id: details.id,
   882	                ref: details.ref,
   883	                status: details.status,
   884	                request_type: details.request_type,
   885	                created_at: details.created_at
   886	            },
   887	            package_code: data?.data?.package_code,
   888	            package_id: data?.data?.package_id,
   889	            message: data?.message
   890	        };
   891	    }
   892	}
   893	
   894	class AmeexAdapter {
   895	    constructor(credentials) {
   896	        this.baseUrl = credentials.api_base_url || 'https://api.ameex.app';
   897	        // AMEEX uses business_id as C-Api-Id and api_key as C-Api-Key
   898	        this.apiId = credentials.business_id || credentials.api_public_key || '';
   899	        this.apiKey = credentials.api_key || credentials.api_secret_key || '';
   900	    }
   901	
   902	    getAuthHeaders() {
   903	        return {
   904	            'C-Api-Id': this.apiId,
   905	            'C-Api-Key': this.apiKey,
   906	            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
   907	        };
   908	    }
   909	
   910	    async trackParcel(trackingCode, externalId) {
   911	        // AMEEX API uses /customer/Delivery/Parcels/Tracking?ParcelCode={code}
   912	        if (!trackingCode) {
   913	            throw new Error('AMEEX tracking requires tracking code');
   914	        }
   915	
   916	        console.log('AMEEX Tracking Request:', {
   917	            url: `${this.baseUrl}/customer/Delivery/Parcels/Tracking?ParcelCode=${trackingCode}`,
   918	            headers: {
   919	                'C-Api-Id': this.apiId,
   920	                'C-Api-Key': this.apiKey ? '***' : 'MISSING'
   921	            }
   922	        });

 // --- Stock error helper ---
 const friendlyStockError = (error: any): string => {
     const m = String(error?.message || error || '');
     if (m.includes('insufficient_stock')) return "Not enough stock to confirm this order. Restock the product or reduce the quantity.";
     if (m.includes('confirm_via_update')) return "Could not create a confirmed order directly. Please try again.";
     return m || 'Something went wrong.';
 };
 
 const computeShortLines = (order: any, productMap: Map<string, number>, accessoryMap: Map<string, number>) => {
     const lines: { name: string; needed: number; have: number }[] = [];
     (order?.items || []).forEach((it: any) => {
         if (it.product_id && Number(it.quantity) > 0) {
             const have = productMap.get(it.product_id) ?? 0;
             if (Number(it.quantity) > have) lines.push({ name: it.product?.name || 'Produit', needed: Number(it.quantity), have });
         }
         if (it.accessory_id && Number(it.accessory_quantity) > 0) {
             const have = accessoryMap.get(it.accessory_id) ?? 0;
             if (Number(it.accessory_quantity) > have) lines.push({ name: it.accessory?.name ? `${it.accessory.name} (accessoire)` : 'Accessoire', needed: Number(it.accessory_quantity), have });
         }
     });
     return lines;
 };
 
 // Group an exchange's related orders into ONE connected block: the returned exchange
 // leg(s) (replace=true), then any additional-sale leg(s) (replace=false that point at the
 // same original), then the original. Tags _exchangeRole + _exchangeFirst/_exchangeLast so

 // partners aren't present in the filtered list.
 function arrangeExchangePairs(list: any[]): any[] {
   const arr = Array.isArray(list) ? list : [];
   const byId = new Map(arr.map((o) => [o.id, o]));
   // legs (return + additional-sale) keyed by the original order they point at
   const legsByOriginal = new Map<string, any[]>();
   for (const o of arr) {

 /**
  * For courier (internal) orders the package status lives on the latest
  * courier_parcels row, not on orders.package_status_id. The orders_list_page
  * RPC computes that fallback server-side (latest courier parcel's embedded
  * status) and ships it as `courier_pkg_fallback` on every slim row.
  */
 function getCourierPackageStatus(order: any): { label: string; color: string | null } | null {
     const fb = order?.courier_pkg_fallback as { label?: string | null; color?: string | null } | null | undefined;
     if (!fb?.label || !String(fb.label).trim()) return null;
     return { label: String(fb.label).trim(), color: fb.color || null };
 }

 // --- Stock error helper ---
 const friendlyStockError = (error: any): string => {
     const m = String(error?.message || error || '');
     if (m.includes('insufficient_stock')) return "Not enough stock to confirm this order. Restock the product or reduce the quantity.";
     if (m.includes('confirm_via_update')) return "Could not create a confirmed order directly. Please try again.";
     return m || 'Something went wrong.';
 };
 
 // D1: items are the SLIM `orders_list_page` shape — flat {product_id,
 // accessory_id, quantity, accessory_quantity, name, code, image_url} where
 // `name` is the PRODUCT name (accessory lines fall back to a generic label —
 // documented contract deviation, the slim row doesn't carry accessory names).
 const computeShortLines = (order: any, productMap: Map<string, number>, accessoryMap: Map<string, number>) => {
     const lines: { name: string; needed: number; have: number }[] = [];
     (order?.items || []).forEach((it: any) => {
         if (it.product_id && Number(it.quantity) > 0) {
             const have = productMap.get(it.product_id) ?? 0;
             if (Number(it.quantity) > have) lines.push({ name: it.name || 'Produit', needed: Number(it.quantity), have });
         }
         if (it.accessory_id && Number(it.accessory_quantity) > 0) {
             const have = accessoryMap.get(it.accessory_id) ?? 0;
             if (Number(it.accessory_quantity) > have) lines.push({ name: 'Accessoire', needed: Number(it.accessory_quantity), have });
         }
     });
     return lines;

 /**
  * For courier (internal) orders the package status lives on the latest
  * courier_parcels row, not on orders.package_status_id. D1: the lookup runs
  * server-side inside `orders_list_page` (LATERAL over courier_parcels) and
  * ships as `courier_pkg_fallback: {label, color} | null` on each slim row.
  */
 function getCourierPackageStatus(order: any): { label: string; color: string | null } | null {
     const fallback = order?.courier_pkg_fallback as { label?: string | null; color?: string | null } | null | undefined;
     if (!fallback?.label || !String(fallback.label).trim()) return null;
     return { label: String(fallback.label).trim(), color: fallback.color || null };
 }

     // --- Date range helper (presets → calendar-day bounds for the RPC) ---
     const getDateRange = useMemo(() => {
         const now = new Date();
         switch (filterTimeRange) {
             case 'today': return { from: startOfDay(now), to: endOfDay(now) };
             case 'yesterday': return { from: startOfDay(subDays(now, 1)), to: endOfDay(subDays(now, 1)) };
             case 'last7': return { from: startOfDay(subDays(now, 6)), to: endOfDay(now) };
             case 'last30': return { from: startOfDay(subDays(now, 29)), to: endOfDay(now) };
             case 'last90': return { from: startOfDay(subDays(now, 89)), to: endOfDay(now) };
             case 'ytd': return { from: startOfYear(now), to: endOfDay(now) };
             case 'thisMonth': return { from: startOfMonth(now), to: endOfDay(now) };
             case 'lastMonth': { const lm = subMonths(now, 1); return { from: startOfMonth(lm), to: endOfMonth(lm) }; }
             case 'custom': return filterCustomRange?.from && filterCustomRange?.to ? { from: startOfDay(filterCustomRange.from), to: endOfDay(filterCustomRange.to) } : null;
             default: return null;
         }
     }, [filterTimeRange, filterCustomRange]);

   255	function orderCompanyIsColiaty(
   256	    companies: { id: string; provider_type?: string | null }[] | undefined,
   257	    companyId: string | null | undefined
   258	) {
   259	    if (!companyId || !companies?.length) return false;
   260	    const row = companies.find((c) => c.id === companyId);
   261	    return !!row?.provider_type && String(row.provider_type).toLowerCase().includes('coliaty');
   262	}

   264	function orderCompanyIsAmeex(
   265	    companies: { id: string; provider_type?: string | null }[] | undefined,
   266	    companyId: string | null | undefined

   268	    if (!companyId || !companies?.length) return false;

   271	    return t === 'ameex' || t.includes('ameex');

   275	function orderCompanyIsColiaty(
   276	    companies: { id: string; provider_type?: string | null }[] | undefined,
   277	    companyId: string | null | undefined
   278	) {
   279	    if (!companyId || !companies?.length) return false;
   280	    const row = companies.find((c) => c.id === companyId);
   281	    return !!row?.provider_type && String(row.provider_type).toLowerCase().includes('coliaty');
   282	}
   283	
   284	function orderCompanyIsAmeex(
   285	    companies: { id: string; provider_type?: string | null }[] | undefined,
   286	    companyId: string | null | undefined
   287	) {
   288	    if (!companyId || !companies?.length) return false;
   289	    const row = companies.find((c) => c.id === companyId);
   290	    const t = String(row?.provider_type || '').toLowerCase();
   291	    return t === 'ameex' || t.includes('ameex');
   292	}

  4137	    // Delvo returns timestamps as either "DD/MM/YYYY HH:mm" (local), ISO strings, or null.
  4138	    parseDelvoDate(raw) {
  4139	        if (!raw) return '';
  4140	        const s = String(raw).trim();
  4141	        // ISO already → keep.
  4142	        if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(s)) return new Date(s).toISOString();
  4143	        // "DD/MM/YYYY HH:mm" or "DD/MM/YYYY".
  4144	        const m = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})(?:\s+(\d{2}):(\d{2}))?/);
  4145	        if (m) {
  4146	            const [_, d, mo, y, hh, mm] = m;
  4147	            return new Date(`${y}-${mo}-${d}T${hh || '00'}:${mm || '00'}:00Z`).toISOString();
  4148	        }
  4149	        const t = Date.parse(s);
  4150	        return isNaN(t) ? '' : new Date(t).toISOString();
  4151	    }

  4153	    async createDeliveryNote() {
  4154	        // BL is created lazily by addParcelsToNote (collection-voucher session POST).
  4155	        const draft = `DELVO-DRAFT-${Date.now().toString(36)}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8)}`;
  4156	        return { reference: draft, provider_response: { draft: true } };
  4157	    }

  4159	    async addParcelsToNote(deliveryNoteRef, parcelCodes, supabase) {
  4160	        const codes = Array.isArray(parcelCodes) ? parcelCodes.filter(Boolean) : [];
  4161	        if (!deliveryNoteRef) throw new Error('Delevo: référence BL requise.');
  4162	        if (codes.length === 0) return { success: true, reference: deliveryNoteRef, parcel_codes: [], inserted: 0 };
  4163	        const isDraft = /^DELVO-DRAFT-/i.test(deliveryNoteRef);
  4164	        if (!isDraft) {
  4165	            // Delevo's BL is created in one shot — no append-after-create endpoint.
  4166	            throw new Error('Delevo: ce BL est déjà émis. Créez un nouveau bon de livraison.');
  4167	        }
  4168	        // 1. Snapshot top BL id BEFORE create so we can identify the new one.
  4169	        const beforeId = await this.fetchLatestBlId();
  4170	
  4171	        // 2. POST /client/collection-voucher/post/add (session + JSON-string colis).
  4172	        const body = new URLSearchParams();
  4173	        body.set('colis', JSON.stringify(codes));
  4174	        body.set('request_pickup', 'on');
  4175	        const addResp = await this.sessionFetch('/client/collection-voucher/post/add', 'POST', body);
  4176	        if (!addResp.ok || addResp.data?.code !== 'ok') {
  4177	            const msg = addResp.data?.message || JSON.stringify(addResp.data || addResp.raw || {}).substring(0, 300);
  4178	            throw new Error(`Delevo: création BL échouée (HTTP ${addResp.status}): ${msg}`);
  4179	        }
  4180	
  4181	        // 3. Datatable poll for the new BL — scan top 5 in case of concurrent creates.
  4182	        // Each datatable POST also harvests fresh Set-Cookie headers via sessionFetch.
  4183	        let newBl = null;
  4184	        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6 && !newBl; attempt++) {
  4185	            const recent = await this.fetchRecentBls(5);
  4186	            // Pick the highest-id BL that wasn't there before. Falls back to the first
  4187	            // BL if `beforeId` was null (no prior BLs at all).
  4188	            for (const r of recent) {
  4189	                if (!beforeId || r.id > beforeId) { newBl = r; break; }
  4190	            }
  4191	            if (!newBl) await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, 600));
  4192	        }
  4193	        if (!newBl) throw new Error('Delevo: BL créé mais introuvable dans la liste — vérifiez le dashboard.');
  4194	
  4195	        // 4. Persist BL identity onto the delivery_note row.
  4196	        if (supabase) {
  4197	            await supabase.from('company_delivery_notes')
  4198	                .update({ reference: newBl.code, external_id: String(newBl.id) })
  4199	                .eq('reference', deliveryNoteRef);
  4200	        }
  4201	        return {
  4202	            success: true,
  4203	            reference: newBl.code,
  4204	            external_id: String(newBl.id),
  4205	            parcel_codes: codes,
  4206	            inserted: codes.length,
  4207	            changed_reference: true,
  4208	            previous_reference: deliveryNoteRef,
  4209	        };
  4210	    }
  4211

  4215	    }
  4216	    async fetchLatestBl() {

  4252	    async fetchRecentBls(limit) {

  4266	    async deleteNote(reference) {
  4267	        // Delevo blocks deletion when parcels are still attached. We surface their message verbatim.
  4268	        if (!reference || /^DELVO-DRAFT-/i.test(reference)) return { success: true, reference };
  4269	        // Need the BL id (not the BR-XXX code). Fetch via datatable filter.
  4270	        const search = this.buildBlDatatableBody({ length: 5, search: reference });
  4271	        const list = await this.sessionFetch('/client/collection-voucher/post/ajax', 'POST', search);
  4272	        const row = Array.isArray(list.data?.data) ? list.data.data.find((b) => String(b.code) === reference) : null;
  4273	        if (!row?.id) return { success: false, reference, error: 'BL introuvable' };
  4274	        const del = await this.sessionFetch(`/client/collection-voucher/delete/${row.id}`, 'POST', new URLSearchParams({ _method: 'DELETE' }));
  4275	        return { success: del.data?.code === 'ok', reference, error: del.data?.message };
  4276	    }
  4277	
  4278	    async deleteParcel(parcelCode) {
  4279	        // Delevo's public API has no parcel-cancel endpoint. Surface gracefully.
  4280	        return { success: true, parcel_code: parcelCode, note: 'Delevo: utilisez le dashboard pour annuler un colis.' };
  4281	    }
  4282	
  4283	    async getPrintUrl(deliveryNoteRef, labelFormat, _printType, _options) {
  4284	        // Format mapping (verified live): A4 → /0/1, thermal 10×10 → /1
  4285	        const fmt = String(labelFormat || 'a4').toLowerCase();
  4286	        const opts = _options || {};
  4287	        if (!deliveryNoteRef || /^DELVO-DRAFT-/i.test(deliveryNoteRef)) {
  4288	            throw new Error('Delevo: BL non émis. Validez le BL avant l\'impression.');
  4289	        }
  4290	        // We need the numeric BL id, not the BR-XXX code. external_id on the note
  4291	        // stores the id; if missing, look it up by code.
  4292	        let blId = String(opts.external_id || '').trim();
  4293	        if (!/^\d+$/.test(blId)) {
  4294	            // Try: opts.supabase + opts.noteId.
  4295	            if (opts.supabase && opts.noteId) {
  4296	                const { data } = await opts.supabase
  4297	                    .from('company_delivery_notes')
  4298	                    .select('external_id, reference')
  4299	                    .eq('id', opts.noteId)
  4300	                    .maybeSingle();
  4301	                if (data?.external_id && /^\d+$/.test(String(data.external_id))) blId = String(data.external_id);
  4302	            }
  4303	        }
  4304	        if (!/^\d+$/.test(blId)) {
  4305	            // Last-resort: datatable lookup by reference.
  4306	            const search = this.buildBlDatatableBody({ length: 5, search: deliveryNoteRef });
  4307	            const list = await this.sessionFetch('/client/collection-voucher/post/ajax', 'POST', search);
  4308	            const row = Array.isArray(list.data?.data) ? list.data.data.find((b) => String(b.code) === deliveryNoteRef) : null;
  4309	            if (row?.id) blId = String(row.id);
  4310	        }
  4311	        if (!/^\d+$/.test(blId)) throw new Error(`Delevo: ID BL introuvable pour ${deliveryNoteRef}.`);
  4312	
  4313	        const path = (fmt.includes('thermal') || fmt === '10x10' || fmt === 'thermal_10')
  4314	            ? `/client/collection-voucher/tickets/pdf/${blId}/1`
  4315	            : `/client/collection-voucher/tickets/pdf/${blId}/0/1`;
  4316	        return this.sessionFetchPdf(path);
  4317	    }
  4318	}

codex
The first concrete defect is at the trust boundary: the new mutating edge action inherits a service-role dispatcher that verifies neither the caller’s business nor ownership of the order/parcel. I’m now validating whether the seller gate can also mix a courier tracking code into an otherwise-company `can_track` record, and whether RLS makes the client-side status update observably fail or silently no-op.
