   343	                   ) order by oi.created_at, oi.id) as items
   344	            from public.order_items oi
   345	            left join public.products pr on pr.id = oi.product_id
   346	            left join public.accessories ac on ac.id = oi.accessory_id
   347	            where oi.order_id = k.id
   348	        ) it on true
   349	        -- getCourierPackageStatus fallback: latest courier parcel's embedded status
   350	        left join lateral (
   351	            select ps2.label, ps2.color
   352	            from public.courier_parcels crp
   353	            left join public.package_statuses ps2 on ps2.id = crp.status_id
   354	            where crp.order_id = k.id
   355	            order by crp.created_at desc nulls last
   356	            limit 1
   357	        ) cpf on true
   358	        -- latest tracking code for DISPLAY: max created_at over both parcel kinds,
   359	        -- non-empty codes, NOT note-gated (parity with the list cell)
   360	        left join lateral (
   361	            select t.tracking_code
   362	            from (
   363	                select cpa.tracking_code, cpa.created_at
   364	                from public.company_parcels cpa
   365	                where cpa.order_id = k.id and nullif(cpa.tracking_code, '') is not null
   366	                union all
   367	                select cra.tracking_code, cra.created_at
   368	                from public.courier_parcels cra
   369	                where cra.order_id = k.id and nullif(cra.tracking_code, '') is not null
   370	            ) t
   371	            order by t.created_at desc nulls last
   372	            limit 1
   373	        ) lt on true
   374	        -- getLatestTrackableCompanyParcel: latest company parcel with a code
   375	        left join lateral (
   376	            select cpb.id as parcel_id, cpb.tracking_code, cpb.external_id, cpb.provider_id
   377	            from public.company_parcels cpb
   378	            where cpb.order_id = k.id and nullif(cpb.tracking_code, '') is not null
   379	            order by cpb.created_at desc nulls last
   380	            limit 1
   381	        ) ctc on true
   382	        -- courier-parcel fallback for can_track (any courier parcel with a code)
   383	        left join lateral (
   384	            select crb.id as parcel_id, crb.tracking_code
   385	            from public.courier_parcels crb
   386	            where crb.order_id = k.id and nullif(crb.tracking_code, '') is not null
   387	            order by crb.created_at desc nulls last
   388	            limit 1
   389	        ) ctk on true
   390	    )
   391	    select jsonb_build_object(
   392	        'rows', coalesce((select jsonb_agg(e.row_json order by e.rn) from enriched e), '[]'::jsonb),
   393	        'page_count', (select count(*)::int from kept),
   394	        'next_cursor', case
   395	            when (select count(*) from page) > v_limit then
   396	                (select jsonb_build_object('created_at', k2.created_at, 'id', k2.id)
   397	                 from kept k2 where k2.rn = v_limit)
   398	            end
   399	    )
   400	    into v_result;
   401	
   402	    return v_result;
   403	end;
   404	$function$
   405	;
   104	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.orders_list_page(p_business uuid, p_seller_scope uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_search text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_status uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_status_ids uuid[] DEFAULT NULL::uuid[], p_package uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_package_ids uuid[] DEFAULT NULL::uuid[], p_city uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_source text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_provider uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_date_field text DEFAULT 'date'::text, p_date_from date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_date_to date DEFAULT NULL::date, p_customer_name text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_phone text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_tracking text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_product uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_confirmed_by uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_assigned_to uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_exchange text DEFAULT NULL::text, p_cursor_created timestamp with time zone DEFAULT NULL::timestamp with time zone, p_cursor_id uuid DEFAULT NULL::uuid, p_limit integer DEFAULT 100)
   105	 RETURNS jsonb
   106	 LANGUAGE plpgsql
   107	 STABLE
   108	 SET search_path TO ''
   109	AS $function$
   110	declare
   111	    v_date_field text;
   112	    v_source text;
   113	    v_customer_name text;
   114	    v_phone text;
   115	    v_tracking text;
   116	    v_exchange text;
   117	    v_search text;
   118	    v_fq text;
   119	    v_pq text;
   120	    v_limit integer;
   121	    v_cur_created timestamptz;
   122	    v_cur_id uuid;
   123	    v_result jsonb;
   124	begin
   125	    if p_business is null then
   126	        raise exception 'd1_orders: p_business is required' using errcode = '22023';
   127	    end if;
   128	    -- seller-page security boundary (RLS does NOT scope sellers — recon 03 §5.1)
   129	    if p_seller_scope is not null
   130	       and p_seller_scope is distinct from (select auth.uid())
   131	       and not coalesce(public.current_user_is_business_admin(), false) then
   132	        raise exception 'd1_orders: p_seller_scope not permitted' using errcode = '42501';
   133	    end if;
   134	    v_date_field := coalesce(p_date_field, 'date');
   135	    if v_date_field not in ('date', 'created_at', 'updated_at', 'assignment_date',
   136	                            'confirmation_date', 'annulation_date', 'date_livraison', 'reported_date') then
   137	        raise exception 'd1_orders: invalid p_date_field %', p_date_field using errcode = '22023';
   138	    end if;
   139	    if coalesce(array_length(p_status_ids, 1), 0) > 1000
   140	       or coalesce(array_length(p_package_ids, 1), 0) > 1000 then
   141	        raise exception 'd1_orders: too_many_ids (max 1000)' using errcode = '22023';
   142	    end if;
   143	    if (p_cursor_created is null) <> (p_cursor_id is null) then
   144	        raise exception 'd1_orders: cursor requires both p_cursor_created and p_cursor_id' using errcode = '22023';
   145	    end if;
   146	    v_limit         := least(greatest(coalesce(p_limit, 100), 1), 1000);
   147	    v_search        := nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_search, '')), '');
   148	    v_fq            := public.like_escape(public.fold_text(v_search));
   149	    v_pq            := regexp_replace(regexp_replace(coalesce(v_search, ''), '\D', '', 'g'), '^0+', '');
   150	    -- escape LAST (after fold/digit derivation): metachars in input match literally
   151	    v_search        := public.like_escape(v_search);
   152	    v_source        := nullif(p_source, '');
   153	    v_customer_name := public.like_escape(nullif(p_customer_name, ''));
   154	    v_phone         := public.like_escape(nullif(p_phone, ''));
   155	    v_tracking      := public.like_escape(nullif(regexp_replace(lower(coalesce(p_tracking, '')), '\s+', '', 'g'), ''));
   156	    v_exchange      := case when p_exchange in ('yes', 'no') then p_exchange end;
   157	    -- OR-3: keyset cursor as PLAIN parameters. The old '(p_cursor_created IS NULL OR
   158	    -- (created_at,id) < (…))' shape demotes the row-compare from Index Cond to Filter
   159	    -- under a generic plan (verified on box; the COALESCE-in-SQL idiom does NOT fix it).
   160	    -- Sentinels make page 1 a bare row-compare too: every real row sorts below
   161	    -- (+infinity, max-uuid), so semantics are identical and the Index Cond survives
   162	    -- any plan mode.
   163	    v_cur_created := coalesce(p_cursor_created, 'infinity'::timestamptz);
   164	    v_cur_id      := coalesce(p_cursor_id, 'ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff'::uuid);
   165	
   166	    with page_raw as (
   167	        select o.*
   168	        from public.orders o
   169	        where
   170	          -- >>> D1 SHARED ORDERS FILTER (byte-identical in orders_list_page + orders_kpis — edit BOTH) <<<
   171	          o."DigiCom Business" = p_business
   172	          and o.date_deleted is null
   173	          and o.source_review_status <> 'pending'
   174	          -- seller pool scoping (recon 02 §1: seller_id = uid is the WHOLE seller predicate)
   175	          and (p_seller_scope is null or o.seller_id = p_seller_scope)
   176	          -- #2/#3 status: single id wins; category array applies only when single is null
   177	          and (p_status is null or o.order_status_id = p_status)
   178	          and (p_status is not null or p_status_ids is null
   179	               or o.order_status_id = any (p_status_ids))
   180	          -- #4/#5 package (orders.package_status_id ONLY — parity, recon 01 gotcha 3)
   181	          and (p_package is null or o.package_status_id = p_package)
   182	          and (p_package is not null or p_package_ids is null
   183	               or o.package_status_id = any (p_package_ids))
   184	          -- #6 city
   185	          and (p_city is null or o.master_city_id = p_city)
   186	          -- #7 source (exact case-sensitive — parity)
   187	          and (v_source is null or o.source_name = v_source)
   188	          -- #8 delivery provider: either FK
   189	          and (p_provider is null or (o.courier_id = p_provider or o.company_id = p_provider))
   190	          -- #9/#10 date range on the whitelisted column, Casablanca-local inclusive days;
    95	             AND (SELECT check_user_role(auth.uid(), ARRAY['super admin','admin'])));
    96	
    97	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    98	-- R5 — orders: ownership-scoped policies + money/attribution column freeze
    99	--   Sellers (role 'commercial') legitimately CREATE/EDIT orders and set
   100	--   price/raw_price (order total). price/raw_price do NOT feed seller
   101	--   commission (which = users.commission_price x units gated by is_delivered

   103	--   that DO drive the commission ledger — seller_id and a delivered
   104	--   package_status_id — are frozen for non-admins.
   105	-- ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   106	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS orders_tenant_insert ON public.orders;

   113	  );
   114

   117	  FOR UPDATE TO authenticated
   118	  USING (

   122	          OR courier_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) )
   123	  )
   124	  WITH CHECK (
   125	    "DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id())
   126	    AND ( (SELECT current_user_is_business_admin())
   127	          OR seller_id = (SELECT auth.uid())
   128	          OR courier_id = (SELECT auth.uid()) )
   129	  );
   130	
   131	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS orders_tenant_delete ON public.orders;
   132	CREATE POLICY orders_tenant_delete ON public.orders
   133	  FOR DELETE TO authenticated
   134	  USING (
   135	    "DigiCom Business" = (SELECT current_user_digicom_business_id())
   136	    AND (SELECT current_user_is_business_admin())
   137	  );

   139	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_orders_money_attribution_cols()
   140	RETURNS trigger
   141	LANGUAGE plpgsql SET search_path = public, pg_temp
   142	AS $$
   143	DECLARE
   144	  v_delivered boolean;
   145	BEGIN

   147	  IF public.current_user_is_business_admin() THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;

   152	        USING ERRCODE = '42501';
   153	    END IF;
   154	    IF NEW.package_status_id IS NOT NULL THEN
   155	      SELECT ps.is_delivered INTO v_delivered
    55	LANGUAGE sql

   657	                              or public.fold_text(pr2.code) like '%' || v_fq || '%'))))

   665	revoke all on function public.orders_kpis(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, uuid[], uuid, uuid[], uuid, text, uuid, text, date, date, text, text, text, uuid, uuid, uuid, text) from public, anon;
   666	grant execute on function public.orders_kpis(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, uuid[], uuid, uuid[], uuid, text, uuid, text, date, date, text, text, text, uuid, uuid, uuid, text) to authenticated;
   667	alter function public.orders_kpis(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, uuid[], uuid, uuid[], uuid, text, uuid, text, date, date, text, text, text, uuid, uuid, uuid, text) owner to postgres;
   668	
   669	-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   670	-- RPC 3 — orders_bulk_guarded: bulk status / attribution with the client-side
   671	-- guards moved server-side (recon 01 §5: selectedOrders persists across pages,
   672	-- so the visible-page locked filter cannot protect the write).
   673	--
   674	-- Guards: ids capped at 1000 (WA 024 precedent), per-id skip when the row is
   675	-- invisible to the caller (nonexistent OR foreign-business — RLS folds both
   676	-- into 'not_found'), soft-deleted, or locked (status.is_paid / is_unpaid).
   677	-- SECURITY INVOKER: the orders UPDATE RLS policy applies per row (an RLS-refused
   678	-- update skips as 'not_updatable' instead of silently counting as applied).
   679	--
   680	-- p_action = 'status': mirrors bulkStatusMutation's payload via the status flag
   681	--   columns (the useStatusCategories facade, verified in
   682	--   src/hooks/useStatusCategories.ts):
   683	--     confirmed  (deducts_stock)                          -> confirmed_by_id + confirmation_date
   684	--     cancelled  (is_cancelled & !fake & !double & !blk)  -> canceled_by_id + annulation_date
   685	--     paid       (is_paid)                                -> date_livraison
   686	--     postponed  (is_postponed)                           -> reported_date (p_reported_date, else now())
   687	--   Each row updates inside its own subtransaction so a trigger rejection (e.g.
   688	--   stock shortage from handle_order_status_stock_update on a confirmed status)
   689	--   skips that order only — same isolation the client's per-order loop had.
   690	--   History: NOT inserted here — the client keeps its pre-insert with the custom
   691	--   comment (trigger dedup window), else trg_auto_log_order_status_history logs
   692	--   the automatic row.
   693	-- p_action = 'attribute': seller_id = p_seller (NULL = unassign, like the
   694	--   client), assignment_date stamped when assigning / cleared when unassigning.
   695	--
   696	-- Returns {applied: [id, ...], skipped: [{id, reason}, ...]}.
   697	-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   698	create or replace function public.orders_bulk_guarded(
   699	    p_ids uuid[],
   700	    p_action text,
   701	    p_status uuid default null,
   702	    p_seller uuid default null,
   703	    p_reported_date timestamptz default null,
   704	    p_comment text default null
   705	)
   706	returns jsonb
   707	language plpgsql
   708	volatile
   709	security invoker
   710	set search_path to ''
   711	as $$
   712	declare
   713	    v_uid uuid := (select auth.uid());
   714	    v_now timestamptz := now();
   715	    v_is_confirmed boolean := false;
   716	    v_is_cancelled boolean := false;
   717	    v_is_paid boolean := false;
   718	    v_is_postponed boolean := false;
   719	    v_applied jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   720	    v_skipped jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   721	    r record;
   722	begin
   723	    if p_action is null or p_action not in ('status', 'attribute') then
   724	        raise exception 'orders_bulk_guarded: invalid p_action %', p_action using errcode = '22023';
   725	    end if;
   726	    if p_ids is null or coalesce(array_length(p_ids, 1), 0) = 0 then
   727	        return jsonb_build_object('applied', '[]'::jsonb, 'skipped', '[]'::jsonb);
   728	    end if;
   729	    if array_length(p_ids, 1) > 1000 then
   730	        raise exception 'orders_bulk_guarded: too_many_ids (max 1000)' using errcode = '22023';
   731	    end if;
   732	    if p_action = 'status' then
   733	        if p_status is null then
   734	            raise exception 'orders_bulk_guarded: p_status is required for the status action' using errcode = '22023';
   735	        end if;
   736	        select s.deducts_stock,
   737	               (s.is_cancelled and not s.is_fake and not s.is_double and not s.is_blacklisted),
   738	               s.is_paid,
   739	               s.is_postponed
   740	        into v_is_confirmed, v_is_cancelled, v_is_paid, v_is_postponed
   741	        from public.order_statuses s
   742	        where s.id = p_status;
   743	        if not found then
   744	            raise exception 'orders_bulk_guarded: unknown p_status %', p_status using errcode = '22023';
   745	        end if;
   746	    end if;
   747	
   748	    for r in
   749	        select d.id,
   750	               o.id as found_id,
   751	               o.date_deleted,
   752	               o."DigiCom Business" as biz,
   753	               (coalesce(os.is_paid, false) or coalesce(os.is_unpaid, false)) as locked
   754	        from (select distinct u.id from unnest(p_ids) as u(id)) d
   755	        left join public.orders o on o.id = d.id   -- INVOKER: RLS hides foreign-business rows
   756	        left join public.order_statuses os on os.id = o.order_status_id
   757	    loop
   758	        if r.found_id is null then
   759	            v_skipped := v_skipped || jsonb_build_object('id', r.id, 'reason', 'not_found');
   760	            continue;
   761	        end if;
   762	        if r.date_deleted is not null then
   763	            v_skipped := v_skipped || jsonb_build_object('id', r.id, 'reason', 'deleted');
   764	            continue;
   765	        end if;
   766	        if r.locked then
   767	            v_skipped := v_skipped || jsonb_build_object('id', r.id, 'reason', 'locked');
   768	            continue;
   769	        end if;
   770	        begin

hook: PreToolUse

    39	        "fixed left-[50%] top-[50%] z-50 grid w-full max-w-lg translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] gap-4 border bg-background p-6 shadow-lg duration-200 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-left-1/2 data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-top-[48%] data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-left-1/2 data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-top-[48%] sm:rounded-lg",
    40	        className,
    41	      )}
    42	      {...props}
    43	    >
    44	      {children}
    45	      <DialogPrimitive.Close className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-sm opacity-70 ring-offset-background transition-opacity data-[state=open]:bg-accent data-[state=open]:text-muted-foreground hover:opacity-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:pointer-events-none">
    46	        <X className="h-4 w-4" />
    47	        <span className="sr-only">Close</span>
    48	      </DialogPrimitive.Close>
    49	    </DialogPrimitive.Content>
    50	  </DialogPortal>
    51	));
    52	DialogContent.displayName = DialogPrimitive.Content.displayName;
    53	
    54	const DialogHeader = ({ className, ...props }: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>) => (
    55	  <div className={cn("flex flex-col space-y-1.5 text-center sm:text-left", className)} {...props} />
    56	);

    58

    61	);
    62	DialogFooter.displayName = "DialogFooter";
    63	
    64	const DialogTitle = React.forwardRef<
    65	  React.ElementRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Title>,
    66	  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Title>
    67	>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    68	  <DialogPrimitive.Title
    69	    ref={ref}
    70	    className={cn("text-lg font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight", className)}
    71	    {...props}
    72	  />
    73	));
    74	DialogTitle.displayName = DialogPrimitive.Title.displayName;
    75

    77	  React.ElementRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Description>,
    78	  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof DialogPrimitive.Description>
    79	>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    80	  <DialogPrimitive.Description ref={ref} className={cn("text-sm text-muted-foreground", className)} {...props} />
    81	));
    82	DialogDescription.displayName = DialogPrimitive.Description.displayName;

     8	- **Rendered only when** the order's latest trackable company parcel (reuse `src/lib/orders/getLatestTrackableCompanyParcel.ts`) exists AND its provider is Coliaty (`provider_type === 'coliaty'`). If `provider_type` is not present in the row's parcels embed, ADD it to the `provider:providers(...)` embed in the query that feeds the rows (check `src/lib/orders/fetchOrderById.ts` `ORDER_BY_ID_SELECT` and the page's own list query — add `provider_type` wherever the row parcels are selected; never match on provider `name`).
     9	- **Disabled (not hidden) when** the parcel's current status is terminal: `status_id` belongs to the master package-status set with `is_terminal` OR `is_returned` true. The pages already load package-status masters via existing hooks (look at `usePackageStatusResolver` / `useStatusCategories` usage in these pages — reuse the existing hub/flag mechanism; do NOT create a new query if one exists).
    10

    12	
    13	- **Step 0 — « Type de demande ».** Kicker: `Demande Coliaty`. Two large option cards (the house option-card pattern: `button` with `role="radio"` + `aria-checked`, selected = `border-ant-ink bg-ant-ink/5`, unselected = `border-ant-line hover:border-ant-ink/20`, `rounded-xl p-4 text-left transition`):
    14	  1. **« Corriger les informations »** — description: `Téléphone, destinataire, prix ou note — sans changer la ville.`
    15	  2. **« Changer la destination »** — description: `Nouvelle ville et adresse de livraison. Le statut de la commande passera à « Relancer vers un nouveau client ».`
    16	  Show the parcel context above the cards in one quiet line: tracking code (tabular-nums) + current status badge. Footer: `Continuer` (disabled until a type is picked), `Fermer`.
    17	- **Step 1 — form.** Fields prefilled from the order row (same source fields the order carries: customer phone → `Téléphone`, customer name → `Destinataire`, order price → `Prix (DH)` numeric, note empty by default → `Note` textarea optional).
    18	  - CORRECT_INFORMATION: only those four fields. No city/address.
    19	  - CHANGE_DESTINATION: additionally `Ville de destination` + `Adresse` (required). On entering this step (and only for this type), fetch allowed cities via the edge action `get_allowed_destination_cities` (contract below) with react-query — **`enabled: open && step===1 && type==='CHANGE_DESTINATION'`** (kept-mounted dialog trap: every query in this dialog MUST be gated on `open`; see the OR-11 comment in `src/components/orders/ParcelTrackingDialog.tsx:58-60`). While loading: skeleton rows (house skeletons, not a centered spinner). On error: inline error panel with the server message verbatim + a `Réessayer` button. City control: shadcn `Select` listing the allowed cities (value = the city's `id` when non-null, else its `name`; label = name). `Adresse`: text input. Validation gates `Continuer`: phone non-empty, receiver non-empty, price ≥ 0, and for CHANGE_DESTINATION city picked + address non-empty.
    20	- **Step 2 — result.** Submit fires on entering (mutation, `useMutation` house pattern with `useToast`):
    21	  1. POST edge `create_parcel_change_demand`.
    22	  2. If success AND type === CHANGE_DESTINATION: resolve the order-status id by **code** — `supabase.from('order_statuses').select('id').eq('code','RELAUNCH_NEW').maybeSingle()` (NEVER hardcode the UUID) — then `supabase.from('orders').update({ order_status_id }).eq('id', orderId)` (user RLS applies; DB triggers handle stock hold + history logging).
    23	  3. Invalidate the page's orders list query (find each page's exact query key — admin uses `['orders-page', activeBusinessId]`-style; use whatever key the host page actually uses, passed in as a prop or invalidated via a callback prop `onSuccess` the host provides).
    24	  States on this step:

    26	  - **Success**: green-accented panel: `Demande créée` + `Réf {ref}` (mono/tabular) + `EN ATTENTE chez Coliaty`. For CHANGE_DESTINATION add the line `Statut de la commande mis à jour : « Relancer vers un nouveau client »`.
    27	  - **Partial success** (demand created but the status update failed): amber warning panel: `La demande a été créée (réf {ref}) mais le statut n'a pas pu être mis à jour — modifiez-le manuellement.`
    28	  - **Error**: destructive panel with the Coliaty message verbatim (their 400 messages are already French). Special-case HTTP 403 → `La clé API Coliaty de ce fournisseur n'a pas la permission de créer des demandes de modification. Contactez Coliaty pour l'activer.` Provide `Retour` (back to the form, inputs preserved) + `Fermer`.
    29	  Footer on success: single `Fermer`.

Note: `package_reciever` is Coliaty's spelling — send exactly that key. `provider_id` = the parcel's `provider_id`.

     1	# TICKET E — provider-integration: two new Coliaty actions
     2	
     3	## TASK
     4	In repo `/Users/ismailelqorchi/Desktop/iGrowFast/New Website/iGrowFast` (path contains a space — quote it), add exactly two new actions to the provider-integration edge function (`supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts`, single file, ~4382 lines):
     5	
     6	1. `get_allowed_destination_cities` — data: `{ tracking_code: string }`
     7	2. `create_parcel_change_demand` — data: `{ tracking_code: string, request_type: 'CORRECT_INFORMATION' | 'CHANGE_DESTINATION', package_phone: string, package_reciever: string, package_price: number, package_note: string, package_city?: string | number, package_address?: string }`
     8	
     9	## EXPECTED OUTCOME
    10	- Both actions dispatch through the existing `switch` on `action` (~line 4239-4362), same `{action, provider_id, data}` request contract as existing actions (`create_parcel`, `list_provider_cities` naming style — follow it).
    11	- Both are **Coliaty-only**: if the resolved adapter/provider is not `provider_type === 'coliaty'`, return a 400 JSON error with a clear message (match how other adapter-specific actions like `list_networks`/`list_stores` handle unsupported providers — inspect and mirror that pattern).
    12	- `get_allowed_destination_cities` → new ColiatyAdapter method calling `GET ${baseUrl}/parcel/${tracking_code}/allowed-destination-cities` with the existing `getAuthHeader()` (line ~209, format `Bearer public:secret`). **The response shape is not documented** — normalize defensively: return `{ cities: Array<{ id: number | null, name: string }> }`, accepting any of: array of objects with id/name-like keys (`id`/`city_id`, `name`/`city_name`/`label`/`ville`), array of strings, or wrapped in a `data` key. If nothing parseable, throw with the raw body snippet in the message (truncate ~300 chars).
    13	- `create_parcel_change_demand` → new ColiatyAdapter method calling `POST ${baseUrl}/parcel-change-demand/create` with JSON body: `{ package_code: tracking_code, request_type, package_phone, package_reciever, package_price, package_note, package_city?, package_address? }` (omit city/address keys entirely for CORRECT_INFORMATION). On success return the API's payload verbatim-ish: `{ success: true, demand: { id, ref, status, request_type, created_at }, package_code, package_id, message }` (pull from `data.details` / `data` of the Coliaty 200 response — see API spec below).
    14	- Error handling mirrors the existing Coliaty pattern (~lines 336-359 createParcel): non-ok → parse text, attempt JSON, surface Coliaty's `message` and the field errors in `errors` when present, and **include the HTTP status code** in the thrown/returned error so the frontend can distinguish 403 (API-key permission) from 400 validation errors. Return the error to the caller as JSON `{ error: string, status_code: number, errors?: object }` with the appropriate HTTP status, matching how the function returns errors elsewhere.
    15	
    16	## CONTEXT (verified facts)
    17	- Adapter class: `ColiatyAdapter`, constructor ~line 202-206 reads `api_base_url` (default `https://customer-api-v1.coliaty.com`), `api_public_key`, `api_secret_key` from the `providers` row; `getAuthHeader()` at ~line 209. Existing endpoints are called as `${baseUrl}/parcel/normal` — the stored base URL already carries any `/api/client` prefix, so use the SAME relative-path convention: `/parcel/${code}/allowed-destination-cities` and `/parcel-change-demand/create`.
    18	- Request handler starts ~line 4201; provider row fetched by `provider_id` ~line 4208 using service role.
    19	
    20	### Coliaty API spec (from provider docs)
    21	POST /parcel-change-demand/create. `request_type` values: `CORRECT_INFORMATION` (fix phone/receiver/price/note; city+address NOT required; validation only checks parcel status allows correction) and `CHANGE_DESTINATION` (requires `package_city` + `package_address`; city must be valid AND allowed for the parcel's hub flow; parcel must not be with a livreur). `package_city` accepts integer city ID or exact city-name string. Success 200:
    22	```json
    23	{"success":true,"code":200,"message":"Demande de modification de colis créée avec succès","data":{"package_code":"…","package_id":646970,"details":{"id":2307,"ref":"PCD-…","status":"PENDING","request_type":"CHANGE_DESTINATION","created_at":"…"}}}
    24	```
    25	Errors: 400 `{"title":"City Not Found","message":"Ville introuvable","errors":{"package_city":"…"}}`; 400 City Not Allowed; 400 "Parcel is with livreur - destination change not allowed"; 403 `{"message":"This API key does not have permission to create parcel change demands"}`; 404 "Colis non trouvé". Auth: `Authorization: Bearer {public}:{secret}`.
    26	Companion: GET /parcel/{parcel_code}/allowed-destination-cities returns the allowed city list (shape undocumented — normalize defensively as specified above).
    27	
    28	## CONSTRAINTS
    29	- **The field name `package_reciever` is Coliaty's own misspelling — send it exactly as spelled.**
    30	- Do NOT implement any order-status flip in this edge function. The frontend performs the RELAUNCH_NEW status update under the user's RLS — this is a deliberate design decision; the edge fn stays a pure Coliaty proxy.
    31	- Do NOT run `deno check` (it rewrites `deno.lock`; the verifier handles that). Do not modify `deno.lock`.
    32	- Match the file's existing code style exactly (indentation, error strings, comment density). No refactors of adjacent code.
    33	
    34	## MUST DO
    35	- Add the two adapter methods to `ColiatyAdapter` and the two `case` branches in the dispatch switch.
    36	- Keep the actions' input validation explicit: missing `tracking_code` → 400; `create_parcel_change_demand` with `request_type === 'CHANGE_DESTINATION'` missing `package_city` or `package_address` → 400 before calling Coliaty; invalid `request_type` → 400.
    37	- Report the exact line ranges you added/changed.
    38	
    39	## MUST NOT
    40	- Touch any file other than `supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts`.
    41	- Spawn subagents or other workers.
    42	- Reformat, re-order, or "improve" existing code.

    44	## OUTPUT FORMAT
    45	First line: `DONE` / `DONE_WITH_CONCERNS` / `NEEDS_CONTEXT` / `BLOCKED`. Then: files touched, line ranges, the exact request/response contract of both new actions (so the frontend can rely on it), and any concerns.

    33	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.clear_order_logistics_when_no_parcels()
    34	 RETURNS trigger LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path TO 'public'
    35	AS $function$
    36	DECLARE
    37	  v_confirmed_id uuid;
    38	  v_processed_id uuid;
    39	BEGIN
    40	  IF OLD.order_id IS NULL OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.orders WHERE id = OLD.order_id) THEN
    41	    RETURN OLD;
    42	  END IF;
    43	  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'company_parcels' THEN
    44	    IF OLD.company_delivery_note_id IS NULL THEN RETURN OLD; END IF;
    45	  ELSIF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'courier_parcels' THEN
    46	    IF OLD.courier_delivery_note_id IS NULL THEN RETURN OLD; END IF;
    47	  END IF;
    48	  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.company_parcels WHERE order_id = OLD.order_id)
    49	     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.courier_parcels WHERE order_id = OLD.order_id) THEN
    50	    RETURN OLD;
    51	  END IF;
    52	  SELECT id INTO v_confirmed_id FROM public.order_statuses WHERE code = 'CONFIRMED' LIMIT 1;
    53	  SELECT id INTO v_processed_id FROM public.order_statuses WHERE code = 'PROCESSED' LIMIT 1;
    54	  UPDATE public.orders
    55	  SET tracking_number          = NULL,
    56	      package_status_id        = NULL,
    57	      current_delivery_note_id = NULL,
    58	      processing               = false,
    59	      courier_id               = NULL,
    60	      order_status_id          = CASE
    61	                                   WHEN order_status_id = v_processed_id AND v_confirmed_id IS NOT NULL
    62	                                   THEN v_confirmed_id ELSE order_status_id END
    63	  WHERE id = OLD.order_id;
    64	  RETURN OLD;
    65	END;
    66	$function$;

./CLAUDE.md:30:**Edge functions:** source of truth is repo `supabase/functions/`. Deploy = rsync `supabase/functions/` → box `/opt/supabase-project/volumes/functions/` (bind-mounted). A rsync ALONE does not reliably take effect: the edge runtime caches WARM workers, so a function with recent traffic keeps serving the OLD module. After rsync you MUST `ssh igf 'docker restart supabase-edge-functions'`, wait for health = healthy, then smoke-test. (A first deploy of an *idle* function cold-loads without a restart — which is why an initial deploy can look fine and a redeploy silently doesn't.) The router `volumes/functions/main/index.ts` also needs the restart when the PUBLIC_FUNCTIONS allowlist changes (self-host `VERIFY_JWT` is global).
./AGENTS.md:30:**Edge functions:** source of truth is repo `supabase/functions/`. Deploy = rsync `supabase/functions/` → box `/opt/supabase-project/volumes/functions/` (bind-mounted). A rsync ALONE does not reliably take effect: the edge runtime caches WARM workers, so a function with recent traffic keeps serving the OLD module. After rsync you MUST `ssh igf 'docker restart supabase-edge-functions'`, wait for health = healthy, then smoke-test. (A first deploy of an *idle* function cold-loads without a restart — which is why an initial deploy can look fine and a redeploy silently doesn't.) The router `volumes/functions/main/index.ts` also needs the restart when the PUBLIC_FUNCTIONS allowlist changes (self-host `VERIFY_JWT` is global).

     5	const JWT_SECRET = Deno.env.get('JWT_SECRET')
     6	const SUPABASE_URL = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')
     7	const VERIFY_JWT = Deno.env.get('VERIFY_JWT') === 'true'
     8	
     9	// Create JWKS for ES256/RS256 tokens (newer tokens)
    10	let SUPABASE_JWT_KEYS: ReturnType<typeof jose.createRemoteJWKSet> | null = null
    11	if (SUPABASE_URL) {
    12	  try {
    13	    SUPABASE_JWT_KEYS = jose.createRemoteJWKSet(
    14	      new URL('/auth/v1/.well-known/jwks.json', SUPABASE_URL)
    15	    )
    16	  } catch (e) {
    17	    console.error('Failed to fetch JWKS from SUPABASE_URL:', e)
    18	  }
    19	}
    20	
    21	/**
    22	 * Extract JWT token from Authorization header
    23	 * 
    24	 * Parses the Authorization header to extract the Bearer token.
    25	 * Expects format: "Bearer <token>"
    26	 * 
    27	 * @param req - The HTTP request object
    28	 * @returns The JWT token string
    29	 * @throws Error if Authorization header is missing or malformed
    30	 */
    31	function getAuthToken(req: Request) {
    32	  const authHeader = req.headers.get('authorization')
    33	  if (!authHeader) {
    34	    throw new Error('Missing authorization header')
    35	  }
    36	  const [bearer, token] = authHeader.split(' ')
    37	  if (bearer !== 'Bearer') {
    38	    throw new Error(`Auth header is not 'Bearer {token}'`)
    39	  }
    40	  return token
    41	}
    42	
    43	async function isValidLegacyJWT(jwt: string): Promise<boolean> {
    44	  if (!JWT_SECRET) {
    45	    console.error('JWT_SECRET not available for HS256 token verification')
    46	    return false
    47	  }
    48	
    49	  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    50	  const secretKey = encoder.encode(JWT_SECRET)
    51	
    52	  try {
    53	    await jose.jwtVerify(jwt, secretKey);
    54	  } catch (e) {
    55	    console.error('Symmetric Legacy JWT verification error', e);
    56	    return false;
    57	  }
    58	  return true;
    59	}
    60	
    61	async function isValidJWT(jwt: string): Promise<boolean> {
    62	  if (!SUPABASE_JWT_KEYS) {
    63	    console.error('JWKS not available for ES256/RS256 token verification')
    64	    return false
    65	  }
    66	
    67	  try {
    68	    await jose.jwtVerify(jwt, SUPABASE_JWT_KEYS)
    69	  } catch (e) {
    70	    console.error('Asymmetric JWT verification error', e);
    71	    return false
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  return true;
    75	}
    76	
    77	/**
    78	 * Verify JWT token, handling both legacy (HS256) and newer (ES256/RS256) algorithms
    79	 * 
    80	 * This function automatically detects the algorithm used in the token and applies
    81	 * the appropriate verification method:
    82	 * - HS256: Uses JWT_SECRET (symmetric key)
    83	 * - ES256/RS256: Uses JWKS endpoint (asymmetric public keys)
    84	 * 
    85	 * This fix ensures compatibility with both legacy tokens and newer asymmetric tokens,
    86	 * resolving the "Key for the ES256 algorithm must be of type CryptoKey" error.
    87	 * 
    88	 * @param jwt - The JWT token string to verify
    89	 * @returns Promise resolving to true if verification succeeds, false otherwise
    90	 */
    91	async function isValidHybridJWT(jwt: string): Promise<boolean> {
    92	  const { alg: jwtAlgorithm } = jose.decodeProtectedHeader(jwt)
    93	
    94	  if (jwtAlgorithm === 'HS256') {
    95	    console.log(`Legacy token type detected, attempting ${jwtAlgorithm} verification.`)
    96	
    97	    return await isValidLegacyJWT(jwt)
    98	  }
    99	
   100	  if (jwtAlgorithm === 'ES256' || jwtAlgorithm === 'RS256') {
   101	    return await isValidJWT(jwt)
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  return false;
   105	}

   108	// verify_jwt=false). Self-host's VERIFY_JWT is global; this allowlist restores
   109	// per-function behaviour so external callers (providers, OAuth redirect, public
   110	// signup) reach these while everything else still requires a valid JWT.

   187	    // backoff on every DETERMINISTIC failure — an RLS 42501 (the admin-gated

   119	  reset(): void {
   120	    // reset needs to remove the observer from the mutation because there is no way to "get it back"
   121	    // another mutate call will yield a new mutation!
   122	    this.#currentMutation?.removeObserver(this)
   123	    this.#currentMutation = undefined
   124	    this.#updateResult()
   125	    this.#notify()
   126	  }
   127	
   128	  mutate(
   129	    variables: TVariables,
   130	    options?: MutateOptions<TData, TError, TVariables, TOnMutateResult>,
   131	  ): Promise<TData> {
   132	    this.#mutateOptions = options
   133	
   134	    this.#currentMutation?.removeObserver(this)
   135	
   136	    this.#currentMutation = this.#client
   137	      .getMutationCache()
   138	      .build(this.#client, this.options)
   139	
   140	    this.#currentMutation.addObserver(this)
   141	
   142	    return this.#currentMutation.execute(variables)
   143	  }
   144	
   145	  #updateResult(): void {
   146	    const state =
   147	      this.#currentMutation?.state ??
   148	      getDefaultState<TData, TError, TVariables, TOnMutateResult>()
   149	
   150	    this.#currentResult = {
   150	      mutation: this,
   151	      observer,
   152	    })
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  protected optionalRemove() {
   156	    if (!this.#observers.length) {
   157	      if (this.state.status === 'pending') {
   158	        this.scheduleGc()
   159	      } else {
   160	        this.#mutationCache.remove(this)
   161	      }
   162	    }
   163	  }
   164	
   165	  continue(): Promise<unknown> {
   166	    return (
   167	      this.#retryer?.continue() ??
   168	      // continuing a mutation assumes that variables are set, mutation must have been dehydrated before
   169	      this.execute(this.state.variables!)
   170	    )
   171	  }
   172	
   173	  async execute(variables: TVariables): Promise<TData> {
   174	    const onContinue = () => {
   175	      this.#dispatch({ type: 'continue' })
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    const mutationFnContext = {
   179	      client: this.#client,
   180	      meta: this.options.meta,
   181	      mutationKey: this.options.mutationKey,
   182	    } satisfies MutationFunctionContext
   183	
   184	    this.#retryer = createRetryer({
   185	      fn: () => {
   186	        if (!this.options.mutationFn) {
   187	          return Promise.reject(new Error('No mutationFn found'))
   188	        }
   189	
   190	        return this.options.mutationFn(variables, mutationFnContext)
   191	      },
   192	      onFail: (failureCount, error) => {
   193	        this.#dispatch({ type: 'failed', failureCount, error })
   194	      },
   195	      onPause: () => {
   196	        this.#dispatch({ type: 'pause' })
   197	      },
   198	      onContinue,
   199	      retry: this.options.retry ?? 0,
   200	      retryDelay: this.options.retryDelay,
   201	      networkMode: this.options.networkMode,
   202	      canRun: () => this.#mutationCache.canRun(this),
   203	    })
   204	
   205	    const restored = this.state.status === 'pending'
   206	    const isPaused = !this.#retryer.canStart()
   207	
   208	    try {
   209	      if (restored) {
   210	        // Dispatch continue action to unpause restored mutation
   211	        onContinue()
   212	      } else {
   213	        this.#dispatch({ type: 'pending', variables, isPaused })
   214	        // Notify cache callback
   215	        if (this.#mutationCache.config.onMutate) {
   216	          await this.#mutationCache.config.onMutate(
   217	            variables,
   218	            this as Mutation<unknown, unknown, unknown, unknown>,
   219	            mutationFnContext,
   220	          )
   221	        }
   222	        const context = await this.options.onMutate?.(
   223	          variables,
   224	          mutationFnContext,
   225	        )
   226	        if (context !== this.state.context) {
   227	          this.#dispatch({
   228	            type: 'pending',
   229	            context,
   230	            variables,
   231	            isPaused,
   232	          })
   233	        }
   234	      }
   235	      const data = await this.#retryer.start()
   236	
   237	      // Notify cache callback
   238	      await this.#mutationCache.config.onSuccess?.(
   239	        data,
   240	        variables,
   241	        this.state.context,
   242	        this as Mutation<unknown, unknown, unknown, unknown>,
   243	        mutationFnContext,
   244	      )
   245	
   246	      await this.options.onSuccess?.(
   247	        data,
   248	        variables,
   249	        this.state.context!,
   250	        mutationFnContext,
   251	      )
   252	
   253	      // Notify cache callback
   254	      await this.#mutationCache.config.onSettled?.(
   255	        data,
   256	        null,
   257	        this.state.variables,
   258	        this.state.context,
   259	        this as Mutation<unknown, unknown, unknown, unknown>,
   260	        mutationFnContext,

    30	  is_unpaid: boolean;
    31	  is_cancelled: boolean;
    32	  is_callback: boolean;
    33	  is_fake: boolean;
    34	  is_double: boolean;
    35	  is_blacklisted: boolean;
    36	  is_postponed: boolean;
    37	  is_relaunch: boolean;
    38	  is_return: boolean;
    39	  deducts_stock: boolean;
    40	  excluded_from_delivery_rate: boolean;
    41	  /** Source of truth for "pending confirmation" (RAPPEL retry queue + NEW + REPORTER). */
    42	  pending_confirmation: boolean;
    43	}
    44	
    45	export interface PackageStatus {
    46	  id: string;
    47	  code: string;
    48	  label: string;
    49	  color: string | null;
    50	  ranking: number | null;
    51	  delivery_service_statuses: unknown;
    52	  show_in_courier: boolean;
    53	  show_in_seller_suivi: boolean;
    54	  show_in_seller_confirmation: boolean;
    55	  perf_breakdown: boolean;
    56	  perf_breakdown_phase: string | null;
    57	  // Semantic behavior flags — owner-governable source of truth.
    58	  is_delivered: boolean;
    59	  is_shipped: boolean;
    60	  is_awaiting_pickup: boolean;
    61	  is_returned: boolean;
    62	  is_refused: boolean;
    63	  is_new: boolean;
    64	  is_cancelled: boolean;
    65	  is_postponed: boolean;
    66	  is_recheck: boolean;
    67	  is_terminal: boolean;
    68	  flow_rank: number | null;
    69	  excluded_from_delivery_rate: boolean;
    70	}
    71	
    72	/**
    73	 * Legacy category-name → flag-predicate facade. Each name resolves EXACTLY to the rows
    74	 * the old `category` value covered (verified by parity when the flags were seeded).
    75	 * 'processed' and 'cancelled' are derived (no dedicated flag) to stay exact.
    76	 */
    77	const ORDER_CAT_PRED: Record<string, (s: OrderStatus) => boolean> = {
    78	  new: s => s.is_new,
    79	  confirmed: s => s.deducts_stock,
    80	  processed: s => s.is_confirmed && !s.deducts_stock && !s.is_paid && !s.is_unpaid,
    81	  paid: s => s.is_paid,
    82	  unpaid: s => s.is_unpaid,
    83	  cancelled: s => s.is_cancelled && !s.is_fake && !s.is_double && !s.is_blacklisted,
    84	  fake: s => s.is_fake,
    85	  double: s => s.is_double,
    86	  blacklisted: s => s.is_blacklisted,
    87	  callback: s => s.is_callback,
    88	  relaunch_new: s => s.is_relaunch,
    89	  return: s => s.is_return,
    90	  postponed: s => s.is_postponed,
    91	};
    92	
    93	const PKG_CAT_PRED: Record<string, (s: PackageStatus) => boolean> = {
    94	  delivered: s => s.is_delivered,
    95	  new: s => s.is_new,
    96	  // The single "Attente De Ramassage" status a parcel enters when placed in a
    97	  // provider pickup note (a specific is_shipped sub-status, uniquely flagged).
    98	  awaiting_pickup: s => s.is_awaiting_pickup,
    99	  recheck: s => s.is_recheck,
   100	  refused: s => s.is_refused,
   101	  returned: s => s.is_returned,
   102	  cancelled: s => s.is_cancelled,
   103	  postponed: s => s.is_postponed,
   104	  // Forward "shipped" progression — only ever queried as a group.
   105	  in_transit: s => s.is_shipped,
   106	  out_for_delivery: s => s.is_shipped,
   107	  picked_up: s => s.is_shipped,
   108	  pickup_pending: s => s.is_shipped,
   109	};
   110	
   111	const orderPred = (cat: string) => ORDER_CAT_PRED[cat] ?? (() => false);
   112	const pkgPred = (cat: string) => PKG_CAT_PRED[cat] ?? (() => false);
   113	
   114	export function useStatusCategories() {
   115	  const activeBusinessId = localStorage.getItem('activeBusinessId');
   116	
   117	  const { data: orderStatuses = [] } = useQuery<OrderStatus[]>({
   118	    queryKey: ['order_statuses_cat', activeBusinessId],
   119	    queryFn: async () => {
   120	      if (!activeBusinessId) return [];
   121	      const { data } = await supabase
   122	        .from('order_statuses')
   123	        .select('id, code, label, color, ranking, recheck, active, show_in_quick_status, show_in_seller_confirmation, show_in_seller_suivi, perf_breakdown, perf_breakdown_phase, pending_confirmation, is_new, is_confirmed, is_paid, is_unpaid, is_cancelled, is_callback, is_fake, is_double, is_blacklisted, is_postponed, is_relaunch, is_return, deducts_stock, excluded_from_delivery_rate')
   124	        .order('ranking');
   125	      return (data || []) as OrderStatus[];
   126	    },
   127	    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
   128	  });
   129	
   130	  const { data: packageStatuses = [] } = useQuery<PackageStatus[]>({
   131	    queryKey: ['package_statuses_cat', activeBusinessId],
   132	    queryFn: async () => {
   133	      if (!activeBusinessId) return [];
   134	      const { data } = await supabase
   135	        .from('package_statuses')
   136	        .select('id, code, label, color, ranking, delivery_service_statuses, show_in_courier, show_in_seller_suivi, show_in_seller_confirmation, perf_breakdown, perf_breakdown_phase, is_delivered, is_shipped, is_awaiting_pickup, is_returned, is_refused, is_new, is_cancelled, is_postponed, is_recheck, is_terminal, flow_rank, excluded_from_delivery_rate')
   137	        .order('ranking');
   138	      return (data || []) as PackageStatus[];
   139	    },
   140	    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
   141	  });
   142	
   143	  return useMemo(() => {
   144	    const orderIdMap = new Map<string, OrderStatus>();
   145	    for (const s of orderStatuses) orderIdMap.set(s.id, s);
   146	
   147	    const pkgIdMap = new Map<string, PackageStatus>();
   148	    for (const s of packageStatuses) pkgIdMap.set(s.id, s);
   149	
   150	    const idsWhere = <T extends { id: string }>(rows: T[], pred: (r: T) => boolean) =>
   151	      new Set(rows.filter(pred).map(r => r.id));
   152	
   153	    const confirmedIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.is_confirmed);
   154	    const paidOrderIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.is_paid);
   155	    const unpaidOrderIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.is_unpaid);
   156	    const cancelledIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.is_cancelled);
   157	    const callbackIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.is_callback);
   158	    const pendingIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.pending_confirmation);
   159	    const newOrderIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.is_new);
   160	    const unreachedOrderIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.is_callback);
   161	
   162	    const adminPullbackOrderIds = idsWhere(orderStatuses, s => s.excluded_from_delivery_rate);
   163	    const adminPullbackPackageIds = idsWhere(packageStatuses, s => s.excluded_from_delivery_rate);
   164	    const deliveredPkgIds = idsWhere(packageStatuses, s => s.is_delivered);
   165	    const shippedPkgIds = idsWhere(packageStatuses, s => s.is_shipped);
   166	    const returnedPkgIds = idsWhere(packageStatuses, s => s.is_returned);
   167	
   168	    const preConfBreakdownIds = new Set(
   169	      orderStatuses.filter(s => s.perf_breakdown && s.perf_breakdown_phase === 'before_confirmation').map(s => s.id)
   170	    );
   171	    const postConfBreakdownIds = new Set(
   172	      packageStatuses.filter(s => s.perf_breakdown && s.perf_breakdown_phase === 'after_confirmation').map(s => s.id)
   173	    );
   174	
   175	    const getPreConfBreakdownStatuses = () =>
   176	      orderStatuses.filter(s => s.perf_breakdown && s.perf_breakdown_phase === 'before_confirmation');
   177	    const getPostConfBreakdownStatuses = () =>
   178	      packageStatuses.filter(s => s.perf_breakdown && s.perf_breakdown_phase === 'after_confirmation');
   179	
   180	    const liveBeforeConfStatuses: OrderStatus[] = orderStatuses.filter(s =>
   181	      s.perf_breakdown &&
   182	      s.perf_breakdown_phase === 'before_confirmation' &&
   183	      !s.is_confirmed &&
   184	      !s.is_cancelled,
   185	    );
   186	    const liveAfterConfStatuses: PackageStatus[] = packageStatuses.filter(s =>
   187	      s.perf_breakdown &&
   188	      s.perf_breakdown_phase === 'after_confirmation' &&
   189	      !s.is_delivered &&
   190	      !s.is_returned,
   191	    );
   192	
   193	    return {
   194	      orderStatuses,
   195	      packageStatuses,
   196	
   197	      // Category-name facade (resolves to flag predicates; no `category` column).
   198	      getOrderByCategory: (cat: string) => orderStatuses.find(orderPred(cat)),
   199	      getOrdersByCategories: (cats: string[]) =>
   200	        orderStatuses.filter(s => cats.some(c => orderPred(c)(s))),
   201	      getPkgByCategory: (cat: string) => packageStatuses.find(pkgPred(cat)),
   202	      getPkgsByCategories: (cats: string[]) =>
   203	        packageStatuses.filter(s => cats.some(c => pkgPred(c)(s))),
   204	
   205	      isOrderCategory: (statusId: string, cat: string) => {
   206	        const s = orderIdMap.get(statusId);
   207	        return s ? orderPred(cat)(s) : false;
   208	      },
   209	      isOrderInCategories: (statusId: string, cats: string[]) => {
   210	        const s = orderIdMap.get(statusId);
   211	        return s ? cats.some(c => orderPred(c)(s)) : false;
   212	      },
   213	      isPkgCategory: (statusId: string, cat: string) => {
   214	        const s = pkgIdMap.get(statusId);
   215	        return s ? pkgPred(cat)(s) : false;
   216	      },
   217	      isPkgInCategories: (statusId: string, cats: string[]) => {
   218	        const s = pkgIdMap.get(statusId);
   219	        return s ? cats.some(c => pkgPred(c)(s)) : false;
   220	      },
   221	
   222	      confirmedIds,
   223	      paidOrderIds,
   224	      unpaidOrderIds,
   225	      cancelledIds,
   226	      callbackIds,
   227	      pendingIds,
   228	      newOrderIds,
   229	      unreachedOrderIds,
   230	      adminPullbackOrderIds,
   231	      adminPullbackPackageIds,
   232	      deliveredPkgIds,
   233	      shippedPkgIds,
   234	      returnedPkgIds,
   235	
   236	      preConfBreakdownIds,
   237	      postConfBreakdownIds,
   238	      getPreConfBreakdownStatuses,
   239	      getPostConfBreakdownStatuses,
   240	      liveBeforeConfStatuses,
   241	      liveAfterConfStatuses,
   242	
   243	      getOrderStatusColor: (statusId: string) => orderIdMap.get(statusId)?.color || '#6b7280',
   244	      getPkgStatusColor: (statusId: string) => pkgIdMap.get(statusId)?.color || '#6b7280',
   245	    };
   246	  }, [orderStatuses, packageStatuses]);
   247	}
  2780	            isOneToOne: false

codex
The seller tracking seam itself is sound: the RPC’s `can_track` object prefers a company-parcel lateral and includes its own non-empty `tracking_code`; `latest_tracking_code` cannot substitute a courier code in the current branch. The separate terminal-status gate is not sound because it reads the order-level cache, which any parcel status update can overwrite.

     6	## FIXES (in severity order)
     7	1. **HIGH — response envelope mismatch.** The edge dispatcher wraps every success as `{ success: true, data: {...} }` (provider-integration/index.ts:4539), but the dialog reads top-level: `json.cities ?? []` (ParcelChangeDemandDialog.tsx:138) and `json.demand ?? {}` (:167). Cities list is therefore always empty and the demand ref always blank. Fix in the dialog: unwrap defensively — `const payload = json?.data ?? json;` then read `payload.cities` / `payload.demand`. Error responses are NOT wrapped (`{ error, status_code, errors? }` returned directly) — keep error reads at top level.
     8	2. **MEDIUM — seller may send a courier tracking code to Coliaty.** Seller's pre-existing `adaptRpcRow` (src/pages/seller/Orders.tsx:1410-1418) synthesizes the order's parcel with `tracking_code: r.latest_tracking_code || r.can_track.tracking_code`, where `latest_tracking_code` can be a COURIER parcel's code on mixed-parcel orders; the new feature currently rides that synthetic parcel via `getLatestTrackableCompanyParcel(order)`. Coliaty must only ever receive the COMPANY parcel's code. Fix at the FEATURE's call sites only (do NOT refactor `adaptRpcRow` — other consumers depend on it): inspect the seller RPC row (`can_track` lateral etc.) and derive the dialog's `tracking_code` + `provider_id` (and the menu gating) exclusively from the company-parcel source, mirroring admin's preference order at src/pages/admin/Orders.tsx:3230-3236.
     9	3. **LOW — silent-success hole in the status flip.** ParcelChangeDemandDialog.tsx:183-187 updates `orders.order_status_id` without `.select()`; an RLS-filtered zero-row update reports success. Append `.select('id')` and treat an empty result array as flip-failure → route to the existing partial-success warning path.
    10	4. **LOW — URL encoding.** provider-integration/index.ts:774 interpolates the tracking code into the URL path raw. Wrap with `encodeURIComponent(...)`. One-line change; touch nothing else in the file.
    11	5. **CONSISTENCY — list views lack the action.** Both table views have per-row DropdownMenus with a Track item (src/components/orders/OrdersListView.tsx:~415 area, src/components/orders/SellerOrdersListView.tsx:369-375). On CHANGE_DESTINATION success the order's `order_status_id` is set to the `order_statuses` row with code `RELAUNCH_NEW`, resolved by code at runtime (grep for hardcoded status UUIDs — any hardcoded UUID for this is a FAIL). A failed flip after a successful demand shows an explicit partial-success warning, not silent success.
    14	5. The orders list the user is looking at gets invalidated/refreshed after success.
    15	6. Error paths surfaced: Coliaty 400 messages shown to the user; HTTP 403 gets a dedicated French explanation about API-key permission.
    16	7. `package_reciever` (Coliaty's misspelling) is the field name sent in the payload.
    17	8. French copy inside the dialog; state coverage: loading, error+retry, success, disabled submit until valid.
    18	9. Run the REAL typecheck from the repo root: `npx tsc -b` (a bare `tsc --noEmit` is vacuous in this repo). It must exit 0. Paste the tail of its output.
    19	10. Write-set discipline: `git status --porcelain` — modified files must be within: `src/components/orders/ParcelChangeDemandDialog.tsx`, `src/pages/admin/Orders.tsx`, `src/pages/seller/Orders.tsx`, `src/lib/orders/fetchOrderById.ts`, plus at most one page-level query-definition file and `supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts` (owned by a parallel worker — ignore it, but flag ANY other modified file, especially `deno.lock`).
    20	
    21	## MUST NOT
    22	- Edit any source file. You may run builds/checks only.
    23	- Spawn subagents.
    24	
    25	## OUTPUT FORMAT
    26	First line: `PASS` / `FAIL` / `PASS_WITH_NOTES`. Then a numbered verdict per criterion with file:line evidence, the tsc output tail, and any defects found (exact location + why it fails the criterion).

codex
FAIL

1. HIGH — `supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts:4323,4331,4473`: The service-role dispatcher validates only the JWT signature. It never verifies caller role, business, provider access, parcel ownership, or order ownership. Any authenticated user with a provider UUID and tracking code can modify another seller’s parcel. Fix: resolve the caller from the JWT and require the provider/parcel/order to belong to the caller’s business, with seller ownership or admin authorization.

2. HIGH — `src/pages/admin/Orders.tsx:856-864`, `src/pages/seller/Orders.tsx:831-840`: The terminal gate checks `order.package_status_id`, not the selected Coliaty parcel’s `status_id`. That order field is overwritten by status changes from any company or courier parcel. On a mixed-parcel order, a non-terminal courier update can enable modification of a terminal Coliaty parcel. Fix: include the selected company parcel’s `status_id` in `can_track` and gate against that exact status.

3. HIGH — `supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts:869-890`, `src/components/orders/ParcelChangeDemandDialog.tsx:170-196`: Every JSON HTTP 2xx is converted into `success: true` without checking Coliaty’s `success` flag or requiring `data.details.ref`. An unexpected 2xx or `{success:false}` response becomes “Demande créée”, displays `Réf —`, and CHANGE_DESTINATION still flips the order status. Fix: reject unless upstream explicitly reports success and provides a non-empty demand reference.

4. MEDIUM — `supabase/functions/provider-integration/index.ts:4473-4484`: Edge validation omits the common required fields. Direct callers can send missing/blank phone and receiver, null/string/negative price, or whitespace-only destination address and the request reaches Coliaty. Fix: validate trimmed strings, finite non-negative price, and valid destination values before calling the adapter.

5. MEDIUM — `src/components/orders/ParcelChangeDemandDialog.tsx:236,538-541`: Only the footer close button is disabled while submitting. The built-in X, Escape, and outside click still close the dialog while the mutation continues, so an apparent cancellation can later create the demand and flip the status. Fix: reject `onOpenChange(false)` while pending and prevent Escape/outside dismissal.

6. MEDIUM — `src/components/orders/ParcelChangeDemandDialog.tsx:226-230,510-514`: The error retry blindly repeats a non-idempotent create request. If Coliaty committed the first request but the response was lost, “Retour” followed by another submit can create a duplicate demand. Fix: add a stable idempotency key/server-side operation record or reconcile ambiguous failures before allowing retry.

Verified non-findings: the seller gate currently cannot substitute a courier tracking code into the Coliaty request; CORRECT_INFORMATION and non-2xx failures do not enter the status-flip branch; zero-row order updates are detected; the cities query key includes provider and tracking code and is gated while closed.
